<file path="src/app/admin/page.tsx">
import { Suspense } from 'react';
import { connection } from 'next/server';
import { getSessionAction, getAdminDashboardDataAction } from '@/app/actions/adminActions';
import { AdminClientWrapper } from '@/components/admin/AdminClientWrapper';
import type { Metadata } from 'next';
import { Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: 'Admin Control Center - Team Hub',
  description: 'Team Hub management portal for sections, tools, calendar, translations, and team access PINs',
};

async function AdminContent() {
  await connection();
  const sessionRes = await getSessionAction();
  const session = sessionRes.success && sessionRes.session ? sessionRes.session : null;

  let dashboardData = null;
  if (session) {
    const dataRes = await getAdminDashboardDataAction();
    if (dataRes.success && dataRes.data) {
      dashboardData = dataRes.data;
    }
  }

  return (
    <AdminClientWrapper
      initialSession={session}
      initialData={dashboardData}
    />
  );
}

export default function AdminPage() {
  return (
    <Suspense
      fallback={
        <div className="min-h-[85vh] flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3">
            <Loader2 className="w-8 h-8 animate-spin text-amber-500" />
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-500">Checking credentials...</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      }
    >
      <AdminContent />
    </Suspense>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/admin/AdminClientWrapper.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { AdminPinLogin } from './AdminPinLogin';
import { AdminDashboard } from './AdminDashboard';
import { getAdminDashboardDataAction } from '@/app/actions/adminActions';
import type { AdminSession, AdminDashboardData } from '@/types/app';
import { Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';

interface AdminClientWrapperProps {
  initialSession: AdminSession | null;
  initialData: AdminDashboardData | null;
}

export function AdminClientWrapper({
  initialSession,
  initialData,
}: AdminClientWrapperProps) {
  const [session, setSession] = useState<AdminSession | null>(initialSession);
  const [data, setData] = useState<AdminDashboardData | null>(initialData);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const handleLoginSuccess = async (newSession: AdminSession) => {
    setSession(newSession);
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await getAdminDashboardDataAction();
      if (res.success && res.data) {
        setData(res.data);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('[AdminClientWrapper] Failed to load dashboard data:', err);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleLogoutSuccess = () => {
    setSession(null);
    setData(null);
  };

  // If not logged in: display sleek PIN keypad
  if (!session) {
    return <AdminPinLogin onLoginSuccess={handleLoginSuccess} />;
  }

  // If loading data after login
  if (loading || !data) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-[80vh] flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-4">
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3">
          <Loader2 className="w-8 h-8 animate-spin text-amber-500" />
          <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-500">Loading admin control center...</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // Once logged in: display full dashboard
  return (
    <AdminDashboard
      session={session}
      initialSections={data.sections}
      initialTools={data.tools}
      initialCalendar={data.calendar}
      initialDictionary={data.dictionary}
      initialPins={data.pins || []}
      onLogoutSuccess={handleLogoutSuccess}
    />
  );
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/admin/AdminDashboard.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  FolderKanban,
  Calendar,
  Languages,
  KeyRound,
  CheckCircle2,
  AlertCircle,
  X,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { AdminTopBar } from './AdminTopBar';
import { TabSectionsTools } from './TabSectionsTools';
import { TabCalendar } from './TabCalendar';
import { TabDictionary } from './TabDictionary';
import { TabAccessPins } from './TabAccessPins';
import { logoutPinAction } from '@/app/actions/adminActions';
import type { Section, Tool, TeamCalendar, UiDictionary, AccessPin, AdminSession } from '@/types/app';

export interface AdminDashboardProps {
  session: AdminSession;
  initialSections: Section[];
  initialTools: Tool[];
  initialCalendar: TeamCalendar;
  initialDictionary: UiDictionary[];
  initialPins?: AccessPin[];
  onLogoutSuccess: () => void;
}

export type AdminTab = 'sections_tools' | 'calendar' | 'dictionary' | 'pins';

export function AdminDashboard({
  session,
  initialSections,
  initialTools,
  initialCalendar,
  initialDictionary,
  initialPins = [],
  onLogoutSuccess,
}: AdminDashboardProps) {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<AdminTab>('sections_tools');
  const [loggingOut, setLoggingOut] = useState(false);

  // Global state across dashboard tabs
  const [sections, setSections] = useState<Section[]>(initialSections);
  const [tools, setTools] = useState<Tool[]>(initialTools);
  const [calendar, setCalendar] = useState<TeamCalendar>(initialCalendar);
  const [dictionary, setDictionary] = useState<UiDictionary[]>(initialDictionary);
  const [pins, setPins] = useState<AccessPin[]>(initialPins);

  // Notification Toast State
  const [toast, setToast] = useState<{ message: string; isError?: boolean } | null>(null);

  const notify = (message: string, isError = false) => {
    setToast({ message, isError });
    setTimeout(() => {
      setToast((curr) => (curr?.message === message ? null : curr));
    }, 3500);
  };

  const handleLogout = async () => {
    setLoggingOut(true);
    try {
      await logoutPinAction();
      onLogoutSuccess();
    } catch {
      onLogoutSuccess();
    } finally {
      setLoggingOut(false);
    }
  };

  // Role-based visible tabs definition
  const tabs: { id: AdminTab; label: string; icon: React.ReactNode; allowedRoles: string[] }[] = [
    {
      id: 'sections_tools',
      label: 'Sections & Tools',
      icon: <FolderKanban className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-500" />,
      allowedRoles: ['editor', 'admin', 'superadmin'],
    },
    {
      id: 'calendar',
      label: 'Monthly Calendar',
      icon: <Calendar className="w-4 h-4 text-blue-500" />,
      allowedRoles: ['admin', 'superadmin'],
    },
    {
      id: 'dictionary',
      label: 'UI Text & Translations',
      icon: <Languages className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-500" />,
      allowedRoles: ['admin', 'superadmin'],
    },
    {
      id: 'pins',
      label: 'PIN & Access Control',
      icon: <KeyRound className="w-4 h-4 text-purple-500" />,
      allowedRoles: ['superadmin'],
    },
  ];

  const visibleTabs = tabs.filter((t) => t.allowedRoles.includes(session.role));

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-950 text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-50 transition-colors">
      <AdminTopBar session={session} onLogout={handleLogout} loggingOut={loggingOut} />

      <main className="flex-1 w-full max-w-7xl mx-auto px-3 sm:px-6 py-4 sm:py-6 space-y-6">
        {/* Navigation Tabs Bar */}
        <div className="flex overflow-x-auto no-scrollbar rounded-2xl bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 p-1.5 border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 shadow-sm gap-1">
          {visibleTabs.map((t) => {
            const isActive = activeTab === t.id;
            return (
              <button
                key={t.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setActiveTab(t.id)}
                className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-3.5 sm:px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-all whitespace-nowrap select-none ${
                  isActive
                    ? 'bg-zinc-900 text-white dark:bg-white dark:text-zinc-950 shadow-sm scale-[1.01]'
                    : 'text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-950 dark:hover:text-zinc-100 hover:bg-zinc-100/70 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800/60'
                }`}
              >
                {t.icon}
                <span>{t.label}</span>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        {/* Tab View Panels */}
        <div className="transition-all animate-in fade-in duration-200">
          {activeTab === 'sections_tools' && (
            <TabSectionsTools
              sections={sections}
              setSections={setSections}
              tools={tools}
              setTools={setTools}
              onNotify={notify}
            />
          )}

          {activeTab === 'calendar' && (
            <TabCalendar calendar={calendar} setCalendar={setCalendar} onNotify={notify} />
          )}

          {activeTab === 'dictionary' && (
            <TabDictionary
              dictionary={dictionary}
              setDictionary={setDictionary}
              onNotify={notify}
            />
          )}

          {activeTab === 'pins' && session.role === 'superadmin' && (
            <TabAccessPins
              pins={pins}
              setPins={setPins}
              currentSession={session}
              onNotify={notify}
            />
          )}
        </div>
      </main>

      {/* Floating Notification Toast */}
      {toast && (
        <div className="fixed bottom-6 right-6 z-50 flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-2xl bg-zinc-900 dark:bg-zinc-100 text-white dark:text-zinc-900 shadow-xl border border-zinc-700 dark:border-zinc-300 text-xs font-semibold animate-in slide-in-from-bottom-3 duration-200">
          {toast.isError ? (
            <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-red-400 flex-shrink-0" />
          ) : (
            <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400 flex-shrink-0" />
          )}
          <span>{toast.message}</span>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setToast(null)}
            className="ml-2 p-0.5 rounded-full hover:bg-zinc-800 dark:hover:bg-zinc-200 transition-colors"
          >
            <X className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/admin/EventHighlightModal.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { X, Check, Calendar, AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import { LANGUAGES } from './ToolBasicInfo';
import type { CalendarEvent, LanguageCode } from '@/types/app';

interface EventHighlightModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSave: (event: CalendarEvent) => void;
  event?: CalendarEvent | null;
}

export function EventHighlightModal({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  onSave,
  event,
}: EventHighlightModalProps) {
  const [date, setDate] = useState('');
  const [time, setTime] = useState('');
  const [speaker, setSpeaker] = useState('');
  const [locationOrLink, setLocationOrLink] = useState('');
  const [titles, setTitles] = useState<Record<LanguageCode, string>>({
    en: '',
    bm: '',
    cn: '',
    ta: '',
  });
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<LanguageCode>('en');
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (event) {
      setDate(event.date || '');
      setTime(event.time || '');
      setSpeaker(event.speaker || '');
      setLocationOrLink(event.location_or_link || '');
      setTitles({
        en: event.title?.en || '',
        bm: event.title?.bm || '',
        cn: event.title?.cn || '',
        ta: event.title?.ta || '',
      });
    } else {
      setDate('');
      setTime('');
      setSpeaker('');
      setLocationOrLink('');
      setTitles({ en: '', bm: '', cn: '', ta: '' });
    }
    setError(null);
    setActiveTab('en');
  }, [event, isOpen]);

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!titles.en?.trim()) {
      setError('English title is required');
      setActiveTab('en');
      return;
    }
    if (!date.trim()) {
      setError('Event date is required (e.g. Nov 12, 2026)');
      return;
    }

    const newEvent: CalendarEvent = {
      id: event?.id || `event-${Date.now()}`,
      date: date.trim(),
      time: time.trim(),
      speaker: speaker.trim(),
      location_or_link: locationOrLink.trim(),
      title: titles,
    };

    onSave(newEvent);
    onClose();
  };

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-3 sm:p-4 bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in duration-200">
      <div className="relative w-full max-w-md flex flex-col rounded-3xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 shadow-2xl overflow-hidden">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-5 py-4 border-b border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50/70 dark:bg-zinc-900/70">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Calendar className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-500" />
            <h3 className="font-bold text-sm text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50">
              {event ? 'Edit Event Highlight' : 'Add Event Highlight'}
            </h3>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="p-1 rounded-full text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:hover:text-zinc-100"
          >
            <X className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-5 space-y-4">
          {error && (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 p-2.5 text-xs text-red-600 bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-950/40 rounded-xl">
              <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 flex-shrink-0" />
              <span>{error}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">
                Date *
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={date}
                onChange={(e) => setDate(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="e.g. Nov 14, 2026"
                className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">
                Time
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={time}
                onChange={(e) => setTime(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="e.g. 8:00 PM GMT+8"
                className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">
              Speaker / Host
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={speaker}
              onChange={(e) => setSpeaker(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="e.g. Marcus Vance"
              className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">
              Zoom or Map Location Link
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={locationOrLink}
              onChange={(e) => setLocationOrLink(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="https://zoom.us/j/... or Hall Address"
              className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-2 pt-2 border-t border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800">
            <div className="flex rounded-xl bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-800/80 p-1 border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800">
              {LANGUAGES.map((l) => (
                <button
                  key={l.code}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setActiveTab(l.code)}
                  className={`flex-1 py-1 text-[11px] font-semibold rounded-lg transition-all ${
                    activeTab === l.code
                      ? 'bg-white text-zinc-950 shadow-sm dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-50'
                      : 'text-zinc-500 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:text-zinc-200'
                  }`}
                >
                  {l.label}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">
                Title ({activeTab.toUpperCase()}) {activeTab === 'en' && '*'}
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={titles[activeTab] || ''}
                onChange={(e) => setTitles({ ...titles, [activeTab]: e.target.value })}
                placeholder={`Event title in ${activeTab.toUpperCase()}...`}
                className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2 pt-3 border-t border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-500 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
            >
              Cancel
            </button>
            <button
              type="submit"
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-600 text-white text-xs font-bold shadow-sm"
            >
              <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>{event ? 'Update' : 'Add Event'}</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/admin/TabAccessPins.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  KeyRound,
  Plus,
  Eye,
  EyeOff,
  Edit,
  Trash2,
  Crown,
  ShieldCheck,
  Check,
  AlertCircle,
  X,
  Loader2,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { upsertPinAction, deletePinAction } from '@/app/actions/adminActions';
import type { AccessPin, AccessPinRole, AdminSession } from '@/types/app';

interface TabAccessPinsProps {
  pins: AccessPin[];
  setPins: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<AccessPin[]>>;
  currentSession: AdminSession;
  onNotify: (msg: string, isError?: boolean) => void;
}

export function TabAccessPins({
  pins,
  setPins,
  currentSession,
  onNotify,
}: TabAccessPinsProps) {
  const [revealedPins, setRevealedPins] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});
  const [pinModalOpen, setPinModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editingPin, setEditingPin] = useState<AccessPin | null>(null);

  // Form State
  const [label, setLabel] = useState('');
  const [pinCode, setPinCode] = useState('');
  const [role, setRole] = useState<AccessPinRole>('editor');
  const [isActive, setIsActive] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);

  const toggleReveal = (id: string) => {
    setRevealedPins((prev) => ({ ...prev, [id]: !prev[id] }));
  };

  const handleOpenAdd = () => {
    setEditingPin(null);
    setLabel('');
    setPinCode('');
    setRole('editor');
    setIsActive(true);
    setPinModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleOpenEdit = (pin: AccessPin) => {
    setEditingPin(pin);
    setLabel(pin.label);
    setPinCode(pin.pin_code);
    setRole(pin.role);
    setIsActive(pin.is_active);
    setPinModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleToggleActive = async (pin: AccessPin) => {
    if (pin.id === currentSession.id) {
      onNotify('You cannot deactivate your own active session PIN', true);
      return;
    }
    const newActive = !pin.is_active;
    const res = await upsertPinAction({
      id: pin.id,
      label: pin.label,
      pin_code: pin.pin_code,
      role: pin.role,
      is_active: newActive,
    });

    if (res.success && res.data) {
      setPins((prev) => prev.map((p) => (p.id === pin.id ? res.data! : p)));
      onNotify(`Access for "${pin.label}" is now ${newActive ? 'active' : 'deactivated'}`);
    } else {
      onNotify('Failed to update PIN status', true);
    }
  };

  const handleDelete = async (pin: AccessPin) => {
    if (pin.id === currentSession.id) {
      onNotify('Security restriction: You cannot delete your own active session PIN', true);
      return;
    }
    if (!confirm(`Are you sure you want to permanently revoke access for "${pin.label}"?`)) {
      return;
    }

    const res = await deletePinAction(pin.id);
    if (res.success) {
      setPins((prev) => prev.filter((p) => p.id !== pin.id));
      onNotify(`Access PIN for "${pin.label}" deleted`);
    } else {
      onNotify(!res.success ? res.error : 'Failed to delete PIN', true);
    }
  };

  const handleSavePin = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!label.trim()) {
      onNotify('Team leader / editor label is required', true);
      return;
    }
    if (!pinCode.trim() || pinCode.trim().length < 4) {
      onNotify('PIN code must be at least 4 digits', true);
      return;
    }

    setSaving(true);
    const res = await upsertPinAction({
      ...(editingPin?.id ? { id: editingPin.id } : {}),
      label: label.trim(),
      pin_code: pinCode.trim(),
      role,
      is_active: isActive,
    });
    setSaving(false);

    if (res.success && res.data) {
      const saved = res.data;
      setPins((prev) => {
        const exists = prev.some((p) => p.id === saved.id);
        if (exists) return prev.map((p) => (p.id === saved.id ? saved : p));
        return [saved, ...prev];
      });
      setPinModalOpen(false);
      onNotify(`PIN access for "${saved.label}" saved successfully`);
    } else {
      onNotify(!res.success ? res.error : 'Failed to save PIN', true);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Top Header & Add Button */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 pb-4">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50 flex items-center gap-2">
            <KeyRound className="w-5 h-5 text-purple-600 dark:text-purple-400" />
            <span>PIN & Team Access Control</span>
          </h2>
          <p className="text-xs text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400 mt-0.5">
            Super Admin Only: Provision, update roles, or revoke PIN access for leaders & editors.
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={handleOpenAdd}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-purple-600 hover:bg-purple-700 text-white text-xs font-bold shadow-md shadow-purple-600/20 transition-all active:scale-95 self-start sm:self-auto"
        >
          <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          <span>Add New PIN</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* PINs Table */}
      <div className="rounded-3xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 overflow-hidden shadow-sm">
        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse min-w-[650px]">
            <thead>
              <tr className="border-b border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50/70 dark:bg-zinc-800/40 text-[11px] font-bold text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
                <th className="py-3.5 px-5">Team Member / Label</th>
                <th className="py-3.5 px-4">PIN Code</th>
                <th className="py-3.5 px-4">Role</th>
                <th className="py-3.5 px-4">Status</th>
                <th className="py-3.5 px-5 text-right">Actions</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody className="divide-y divide-zinc-200 dark:divide-zinc-800 text-xs">
              {pins.map((pin) => {
                const isCurrent = pin.id === currentSession.id;
                const isRevealed = revealedPins[pin.id];

                return (
                  <tr
                    key={pin.id}
                    className="hover:bg-zinc-50/60 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800/30 transition-colors"
                  >
                    <td className="py-3.5 px-5">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <span className="font-bold text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50">
                          {pin.label}
                        </span>
                        {isCurrent && (
                          <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold bg-purple-100 text-purple-700 dark:bg-purple-950/60 dark:text-purple-300">
                            You (Active Session)
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </td>

                    <td className="py-3.5 px-4">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <span className="font-mono text-xs font-bold tracking-widest text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300">
                          {isRevealed ? pin.pin_code : '••••••'}
                        </span>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => toggleReveal(pin.id)}
                          className="p-1 rounded-lg text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:hover:text-zinc-100 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
                          title={isRevealed ? 'Hide PIN' : 'Reveal PIN'}
                        >
                          {isRevealed ? <EyeOff className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> : <Eye className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </td>

                    <td className="py-3.5 px-4">
                      <span
                        className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px] font-semibold border ${
                          pin.role === 'superadmin'
                            ? 'bg-purple-100 text-purple-800 border-purple-200 dark:bg-purple-950/60 dark:text-purple-300 dark:border-purple-800'
                            : pin.role === 'admin'
                            ? 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-800 border-blue-200 dark:bg-blue-950/60 dark:text-blue-300 dark:border-blue-800'
                            : 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-200 dark:bg-emerald-950/60 dark:text-emerald-300 dark:border-emerald-800'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {pin.role === 'superadmin' ? (
                          <Crown className="w-3 h-3" />
                        ) : (
                          <ShieldCheck className="w-3 h-3" />
                        )}
                        <span className="capitalize">{pin.role}</span>
                      </span>
                    </td>

                    <td className="py-3.5 px-4">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleToggleActive(pin)}
                        disabled={isCurrent}
                        className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold border transition-all ${
                          pin.is_active
                            ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 dark:text-emerald-300 dark:border-emerald-900'
                            : 'bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-500 border-zinc-200 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:border-zinc-700'
                        } disabled:opacity-60 disabled:cursor-not-allowed`}
                        title={isCurrent ? 'Cannot deactivate self' : 'Click to toggle status'}
                      >
                        <span>{pin.is_active ? 'Active' : 'Inactive'}</span>
                      </button>
                    </td>

                    <td className="py-3.5 px-5 text-right">
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-1.5">
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => handleOpenEdit(pin)}
                          className="p-1.5 rounded-xl text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 transition-colors"
                          title="Edit PIN or Role"
                        >
                          <Edit className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                        </button>

                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => handleDelete(pin)}
                          disabled={isCurrent}
                          className="p-1.5 rounded-xl text-red-600 dark:text-red-400 hover:bg-red-50 dark:hover:bg-red-950/40 transition-colors disabled:opacity-20 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
                          title={isCurrent ? 'Cannot delete own active PIN' : 'Delete PIN'}
                        >
                          <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                );
              })}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>
      {/* Add / Edit PIN Modal */}
      {pinModalOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-3 sm:p-4 bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in duration-200">
          <div className="relative w-full max-w-md rounded-3xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 shadow-2xl overflow-hidden">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-5 py-4 border-b border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50/70 dark:bg-zinc-900/70">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <KeyRound className="w-4 h-4 text-purple-600 dark:text-purple-400" />
                <h3 className="font-bold text-sm text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50">
                  {editingPin ? 'Edit Team PIN' : 'Add New Team PIN'}
                </h3>
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setPinModalOpen(false)}
                className="p-1 rounded-full text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:hover:text-zinc-100"
              >
                <X className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>
            </div>

            <form onSubmit={handleSavePin} className="p-5 space-y-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">
                  Team Leader / Editor Name *
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={label}
                  onChange={(e) => setLabel(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="e.g. Marcus Vance - Regional Lead"
                  className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-purple-500"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">
                  PIN Code (Digits) *
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={pinCode}
                  onChange={(e) => setPinCode(e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 8))}
                  placeholder="e.g. 654321"
                  className="w-full font-mono text-sm tracking-widest rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-purple-500"
                />
                <p className="text-[10px] text-zinc-400 mt-1">Numbers only, recommended 6 digits</p>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1.5">
                  Role Permission Level *
                </label>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
                  {(['editor', 'admin', 'superadmin'] as AccessPinRole[]).map((r) => {
                    const selected = role === r;
                    return (
                      <button
                        key={r}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setRole(r)}
                        className={`py-2 px-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold border transition-all text-center capitalize ${
                          selected
                            ? 'bg-purple-600 text-white border-purple-600 shadow-sm'
                            : 'bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-700'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {r === 'superadmin' ? 'Super Admin' : r}
                      </button>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 pt-1">
                <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 cursor-pointer">
                  <input
                    type="checkbox"
                    checked={isActive}
                    onChange={(e) => setIsActive(e.target.checked)}
                    className="rounded text-purple-600 focus:ring-purple-500"
                  />
                  <span>Access is Active</span>
                </label>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2 pt-3 border-t border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setPinModalOpen(false)}
                  disabled={saving}
                  className="px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-500 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
                >
                  Cancel
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={saving}
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-purple-600 hover:bg-purple-700 text-white text-xs font-bold shadow-sm disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  {saving ? (
                    <>
                      <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin" />
                      <span>Saving...</span>
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                      <span>{editingPin ? 'Update PIN' : 'Create PIN'}</span>
                    </>
                  )}
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/admin/TabDictionary.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  Languages,
  Search,
  Save,
  Plus,
  Loader2,
  Check,
  AlertCircle,
  X,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { updateDictionaryAction } from '@/app/actions/adminActions';
import type { UiDictionary } from '@/types/app';

interface TabDictionaryProps {
  dictionary: UiDictionary[];
  setDictionary: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<UiDictionary[]>>;
  onNotify: (msg: string, isError?: boolean) => void;
}

export function TabDictionary({
  dictionary,
  setDictionary,
  onNotify,
}: TabDictionaryProps) {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [filterMissing, setFilterMissing] = useState(false);
  const [savingKey, setSavingKey] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [savingAll, setSavingAll] = useState(false);

  // New key modal state
  const [newKeyModalOpen, setNewKeyModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [newKey, setNewKey] = useState('');
  const [newEn, setNewEn] = useState('');
  const [newBm, setNewBm] = useState('');
  const [newCn, setNewCn] = useState('');
  const [newTa, setNewTa] = useState('');

  const handleCellChange = (key: string, field: 'en' | 'bm' | 'cn' | 'ta', val: string) => {
    setDictionary((prev) =>
      prev.map((item) => (item.key === key ? { ...item, [field]: val } : item))
    );
  };

  const handleSaveRow = async (entry: UiDictionary) => {
    setSavingKey(entry.key);
    const res = await updateDictionaryAction(entry);
    setSavingKey(null);

    if (res.success) {
      onNotify(`Translation for "${entry.key}" updated`);
    } else {
      onNotify('Failed to save translation', true);
    }
  };

  const handleSaveAll = async () => {
    setSavingAll(true);
    const res = await updateDictionaryAction(dictionary);
    setSavingAll(false);

    if (res.success) {
      onNotify('All UI translations saved successfully');
    } else {
      onNotify('Failed to save translations', true);
    }
  };

  const handleCreateNewKey = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const cleanKey = newKey.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9_]/g, '_');
    if (!cleanKey) {
      onNotify('Key name is required', true);
      return;
    }
    if (!newEn.trim()) {
      onNotify('English text is required', true);
      return;
    }

    const newEntry: UiDictionary = {
      key: cleanKey,
      en: newEn.trim(),
      bm: newBm.trim(),
      cn: newCn.trim(),
      ta: newTa.trim(),
    };

    const res = await updateDictionaryAction(newEntry);
    if (res.success) {
      setDictionary((prev) => [newEntry, ...prev]);
      setNewKey('');
      setNewEn('');
      setNewBm('');
      setNewCn('');
      setNewTa('');
      setNewKeyModalOpen(false);
      onNotify(`New key "${cleanKey}" added successfully`);
    } else {
      onNotify('Failed to create new key', true);
    }
  };

  const filteredEntries = dictionary.filter((item) => {
    if (filterMissing) {
      const hasMissing = !item.bm?.trim() || !item.cn?.trim() || !item.ta?.trim();
      if (!hasMissing) return false;
    }
    if (!search.trim()) return true;
    const q = search.toLowerCase();
    return (
      item.key.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      item.en?.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      item.bm?.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      item.cn?.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      item.ta?.toLowerCase().includes(q)
    );
  });

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Top Header & Global Actions */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 pb-4">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50 flex items-center gap-2">
            <Languages className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-500" />
            <span>UI Text & Translations Management</span>
          </h2>
          <p className="text-xs text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400 mt-0.5">
            Searchable, editable table of all dictionary strings across English, Malay, Chinese, and Tamil.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 self-start sm:self-auto">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setNewKeyModalOpen(true)}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 hover:bg-zinc-50 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 shadow-sm transition-all"
          >
            <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>Add Key</span>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleSaveAll}
            disabled={savingAll}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500 to-orange-500 hover:from-amber-600 hover:to-orange-600 text-white text-xs font-bold shadow-md shadow-orange-500/20 transition-all active:scale-95 disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            {savingAll ? (
              <>
                <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin" />
                <span>Saving All...</span>
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <Save className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                <span>Save All Changes</span>
              </>
            )}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Filter Bar */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-3 items-stretch sm:items-center justify-between">
        <div className="relative flex-1 max-w-md">
          <Search className="absolute left-3 top-2.5 w-4 h-4 text-zinc-400" />
          <input
            type="text"
            value={search}
            onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Search phrase, translation, or key..."
            className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 placeholder:text-zinc-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500 shadow-sm"
          />
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setFilterMissing(!filterMissing)}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold border transition-all ${
              filterMissing
                ? 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-900 border-amber-300 dark:bg-amber-950/60 dark:text-amber-300 dark:border-amber-800'
                : 'bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-600 border-zinc-200 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:border-zinc-700'
            }`}
          >
            {filterMissing ? 'Showing Missing Translations Only' : 'Filter Incomplete Phrases'}
          </button>
          <span className="text-xs text-zinc-400 font-medium">
            {filteredEntries.length} phrases
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Table Container */}
      <div className="rounded-3xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 overflow-hidden shadow-sm">
        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse min-w-[700px]">
            <thead>
              <tr className="border-b border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50/70 dark:bg-zinc-800/40 text-[11px] font-bold text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
                <th className="py-3 px-4 w-44">Key</th>
                <th className="py-3 px-4">English</th>
                <th className="py-3 px-4">Bahasa Melayu</th>
                <th className="py-3 px-4">中文</th>
                <th className="py-3 px-4">தமிழ்</th>
                <th className="py-3 px-4 w-20 text-right">Action</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody className="divide-y divide-zinc-200 dark:divide-zinc-800 text-xs">
              {filteredEntries.map((row) => {
                const isSaving = savingKey === row.key;
                return (
                  <tr
                    key={row.key}
                    className="hover:bg-zinc-50/60 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800/30 transition-colors"
                  >
                    <td className="py-3 px-4 align-top">
                      <span className="font-mono text-[11px] font-semibold text-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-200 bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-2 py-1 rounded-lg break-all">
                        {row.key}
                      </span>
                    </td>

                    <td className="py-2.5 px-3 align-top">
                      <textarea
                        rows={2}
                        value={row.en || ''}
                        onChange={(e) => handleCellChange(row.key, 'en', e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50/50 dark:bg-zinc-800/60 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-amber-500 resize-none"
                      />
                    </td>

                    <td className="py-2.5 px-3 align-top">
                      <textarea
                        rows={2}
                        value={row.bm || ''}
                        onChange={(e) => handleCellChange(row.key, 'bm', e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50/50 dark:bg-zinc-800/60 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-amber-500 resize-none"
                      />
                    </td>

                    <td className="py-2.5 px-3 align-top">
                      <textarea
                        rows={2}
                        value={row.cn || ''}
                        onChange={(e) => handleCellChange(row.key, 'cn', e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50/50 dark:bg-zinc-800/60 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-amber-500 resize-none"
                      />
                    </td>

                    <td className="py-2.5 px-3 align-top">
                      <textarea
                        rows={2}
                        value={row.ta || ''}
                        onChange={(e) => handleCellChange(row.key, 'ta', e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50/50 dark:bg-zinc-800/60 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-amber-500 resize-none"
                      />
                    </td>

                    <td className="py-2.5 px-4 align-top text-right">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleSaveRow(row)}
                        disabled={isSaving}
                        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 hover:bg-amber-100 dark:bg-amber-950/40 dark:text-amber-300 font-semibold text-[11px] border border-amber-200 dark:border-amber-900 transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"
                        title="Save this row"
                      >
                        {isSaving ? (
                          <Loader2 className="w-3 h-3 animate-spin" />
                        ) : (
                          <Save className="w-3 h-3" />
                        )}
                        <span>Save</span>
                      </button>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                );
              })}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Add New Key Modal */}
      {newKeyModalOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-3 sm:p-4 bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in duration-200">
          <div className="relative w-full max-w-lg rounded-3xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 shadow-2xl overflow-hidden">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-5 py-4 border-b border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50/70 dark:bg-zinc-900/70">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Languages className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-500" />
                <h3 className="font-bold text-sm text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50">
                  Add New Translation Key
                </h3>
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setNewKeyModalOpen(false)}
                className="p-1 rounded-full text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:hover:text-zinc-100"
              >
                <X className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>
            </div>

            <form onSubmit={handleCreateNewKey} className="p-5 space-y-3.5">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">
                  Unique Key (snake_case) *
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={newKey}
                  onChange={(e) => setNewKey(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="e.g. btn_confirm_action"
                  className="w-full font-mono rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">
                  English Text *
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={newEn}
                  onChange={(e) => setNewEn(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="English phrase..."
                  className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-2.5">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">
                    Bahasa Melayu
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={newBm}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewBm(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="BM..."
                    className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500"
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">
                    中文
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={newCn}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewCn(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="中文..."
                    className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500"
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">
                    தமிழ்
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={newTa}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewTa(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="தமிழ்..."
                    className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2 pt-3 border-t border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setNewKeyModalOpen(false)}
                  className="px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-500 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
                >
                  Cancel
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-600 text-white text-xs font-bold shadow-sm"
                >
                  <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>Create Key</span>
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/admin/TabSectionsTools.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  Folder,
  Wrench,
  Plus,
  ArrowUp,
  ArrowDown,
  Eye,
  EyeOff,
  Edit,
  Trash2,
  Star,
  Search,
  Check,
  AlertCircle,
  Video,
  FileText,
  Music,
  Link as LinkIcon,
  Image as ImageIcon,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { SectionModal } from './SectionModal';
import { ToolModal } from './ToolModal';
import { ToolAdminCard } from './ToolAdminCard';
import {
  upsertSectionAction,
  deleteSectionAction,
  reorderSectionsAction,
  upsertToolAction,
  deleteToolAction,
} from '@/app/actions/adminActions';
import type { Section, Tool } from '@/types/app';

interface TabSectionsToolsProps {
  sections: Section[];
  setSections: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<Section[]>>;
  tools: Tool[];
  setTools: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<Tool[]>>;
  onNotify: (msg: string, isError?: boolean) => void;
}

export function TabSectionsTools({
  sections,
  setSections,
  tools,
  setTools,
  onNotify,
}: TabSectionsToolsProps) {
  const [subTab, setSubTab] = useState<'sections' | 'tools'>('sections');

  // Modals state
  const [sectionModalOpen, setSectionModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editingSection, setEditingSection] = useState<Section | null>(null);

  const [toolModalOpen, setToolModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editingTool, setEditingTool] = useState<Tool | null>(null);

  // Tools filter state
  const [selectedSectionFilter, setSelectedSectionFilter] = useState<string>('all');
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');

  // Reorder sections
  const handleMoveSection = async (index: number, direction: 'up' | 'down') => {
    const targetIndex = direction === 'up' ? index - 1 : index + 1;
    if (targetIndex < 0 || targetIndex >= sections.length) return;

    const reordered = [...sections];
    const [moved] = reordered.splice(index, 1);
    reordered.splice(targetIndex, 0, moved);

    const updated = reordered.map((sec, idx) => ({ ...sec, sort_order: idx + 1 }));
    setSections(updated);

    const res = await reorderSectionsAction(updated.map((s) => ({ id: s.id, sort_order: s.sort_order })));
    if (res.success) {
      onNotify('Sections reordered successfully');
    } else {
      onNotify('Failed to save order in database', true);
    }
  };

  // Toggle section visibility
  const handleToggleSectionVisibility = async (sec: Section) => {
    const newVis = !sec.is_visible;
    const updated = sections.map((s) => (s.id === sec.id ? { ...s, is_visible: newVis } : s));
    setSections(updated);

    const res = await upsertSectionAction({ ...sec, is_visible: newVis });
    if (res.success) {
      onNotify(`Section is now ${newVis ? 'visible' : 'hidden'}`);
    } else {
      onNotify('Failed to update visibility', true);
    }
  };

  // Delete section
  const handleDeleteSection = async (sec: Section) => {
    if (!confirm(`Are you sure you want to delete section "${sec.title.en}"? All associated tools will also be deleted.`)) {
      return;
    }

    const res = await deleteSectionAction(sec.id);
    if (res.success) {
      setSections((prev) => prev.filter((s) => s.id !== sec.id));
      setTools((prev) => prev.filter((t) => t.section_id !== sec.id));
      onNotify('Section and tools deleted successfully');
    } else {
      onNotify('Failed to delete section', true);
    }
  };

  // Save section
  const handleSaveSection = async (payload: Partial<Section>): Promise<boolean> => {
    const res = await upsertSectionAction(payload);
    if (res.success && res.data) {
      const saved = res.data;
      setSections((prev) => {
        const exists = prev.some((s) => s.id === saved.id);
        if (exists) {
          return prev.map((s) => (s.id === saved.id ? saved : s));
        }
        return [...prev, saved];
      });
      onNotify('Section saved successfully');
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  };

  // Toggle tool featured
  const handleToggleToolFeatured = async (tool: Tool) => {
    const newFeatured = !tool.is_featured;
    setTools((prev) => prev.map((t) => (t.id === tool.id ? { ...t, is_featured: newFeatured } : t)));

    const res = await upsertToolAction({ ...tool, is_featured: newFeatured });
    if (res.success) {
      onNotify(`Tool ${newFeatured ? 'featured on top' : 'unfeatured'}`);
    } else {
      onNotify('Failed to update tool', true);
    }
  };

  // Toggle tool visibility
  const handleToggleToolVisibility = async (tool: Tool) => {
    const newVis = !tool.is_visible;
    setTools((prev) => prev.map((t) => (t.id === tool.id ? { ...t, is_visible: newVis } : t)));

    const res = await upsertToolAction({ ...tool, is_visible: newVis });
    if (res.success) {
      onNotify(`Tool is now ${newVis ? 'visible' : 'hidden'}`);
    } else {
      onNotify('Failed to update tool visibility', true);
    }
  };

  // Delete tool
  const handleDeleteTool = async (tool: Tool) => {
    if (!confirm(`Delete tool "${tool.title.en}"?`)) return;

    const res = await deleteToolAction(tool.id);
    if (res.success) {
      setTools((prev) => prev.filter((t) => t.id !== tool.id));
      onNotify('Tool deleted successfully');
    } else {
      onNotify('Failed to delete tool', true);
    }
  };

  // Save tool
  const handleSaveTool = async (payload: Partial<Tool>): Promise<boolean> => {
    const res = await upsertToolAction(payload);
    if (res.success && res.data) {
      const saved = res.data;
      setTools((prev) => {
        const exists = prev.some((t) => t.id === saved.id);
        if (exists) {
          return prev.map((t) => (t.id === saved.id ? saved : t));
        }
        return [saved, ...prev];
      });
      onNotify('Tool saved successfully');
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Subtab Toggle & Global Add Buttons */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 pb-4">
        <div className="flex rounded-2xl bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-800/80 p-1 border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setSubTab('sections')}
            className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-all ${
              subTab === 'sections'
                ? 'bg-white text-zinc-950 shadow-sm dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-50'
                : 'text-zinc-600 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:text-zinc-200'
            }`}
          >
            <Folder className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-500" />
            <span>Manage Sections ({sections.length})</span>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setSubTab('tools')}
            className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-all ${
              subTab === 'tools'
                ? 'bg-white text-zinc-950 shadow-sm dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-50'
                : 'text-zinc-600 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:text-zinc-200'
            }`}
          >
            <Wrench className="w-4 h-4 text-blue-500" />
            <span>Manage Tools ({tools.length})</span>
          </button>
        </div>

        <div>
          {subTab === 'sections' ? (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                setEditingSection(null);
                setSectionModalOpen(true);
              }}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-600 text-white text-xs font-bold shadow-sm transition-all active:scale-95"
            >
              <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>Add Section</span>
            </button>
          ) : (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                setEditingTool(null);
                setToolModalOpen(true);
              }}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500 to-orange-500 hover:from-amber-600 hover:to-orange-600 text-white text-xs font-bold shadow-sm transition-all active:scale-95"
            >
              <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>Add Tool</span>
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {subTab === 'sections' ? (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-zinc-500">
            <span>Drag or use arrows to reorder sections. Changes save immediately.</span>
            <span>{sections.length} total sections</span>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-2.5">
            {sections.map((sec, idx) => {
              const secToolsCount = tools.filter((t) => t.section_id === sec.id).length;
              return (
                <div
                  key={sec.id}
                  className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between p-3.5 sm:p-4 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-all gap-3"
                >
                  <div className="flex items-start sm:items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
                    <div className="flex flex-col gap-0.5 items-center justify-center">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleMoveSection(idx, 'up')}
                        disabled={idx === 0}
                        aria-label="Move Up"
                        className="p-1 rounded-lg text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:hover:text-zinc-100 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 disabled:opacity-20"
                      >
                        <ArrowUp className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                      </button>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleMoveSection(idx, 'down')}
                        disabled={idx === sections.length - 1}
                        aria-label="Move Down"
                        className="p-1 rounded-lg text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:hover:text-zinc-100 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 disabled:opacity-20"
                      >
                        <ArrowDown className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                      </button>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400 font-bold flex-shrink-0">
                      <Folder className="w-5 h-5" />
                    </div>

                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                        <span className="font-bold text-sm text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100">
                          {sec.title?.en || 'Untitled'}
                        </span>
                        <span className="text-xs text-zinc-400">/{sec.slug}</span>
                        <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-semibold bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400">
                          {secToolsCount} {secToolsCount === 1 ? 'tool' : 'tools'}
                        </span>
                      </div>

                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mt-1 text-[11px] text-zinc-500 flex-wrap">
                        {sec.title?.bm && <span>BM: {sec.title.bm}</span>}
                        {sec.title?.cn && <span>• 中文: {sec.title.cn}</span>}
                        {sec.title?.ta && <span>• தமிழ்: {sec.title.ta}</span>}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 self-end sm:self-center">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleToggleSectionVisibility(sec)}
                      className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold border transition-all ${
                        sec.is_visible
                          ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 dark:text-emerald-300 dark:border-emerald-900'
                          : 'bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-500 border-zinc-200 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:border-zinc-700'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {sec.is_visible ? <Eye className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> : <EyeOff className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
                      <span>{sec.is_visible ? 'Visible' : 'Hidden'}</span>
                    </button>

                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => {
                        setEditingSection(sec);
                        setSectionModalOpen(true);
                      }}
                      className="p-1.5 rounded-xl text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 transition-colors"
                      title="Edit Section"
                    >
                      <Edit className="w-4 h-4" />
                    </button>

                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleDeleteSection(sec)}
                      className="p-1.5 rounded-xl text-red-600 dark:text-red-400 hover:bg-red-50 dark:hover:bg-red-950/40 transition-colors"
                      title="Delete Section"
                    >
                      <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {/* Filter Bar */}
          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-3 items-stretch sm:items-center justify-between">
            <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-2 flex-1">
              <div className="relative flex-1">
                <Search className="absolute left-3 top-2.5 w-4 h-4 text-zinc-400" />
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={searchQuery}
                  onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Search tools by title or speaker..."
                  className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 placeholder:text-zinc-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500 shadow-sm"
                />
              </div>

              <select
                value={selectedSectionFilter}
                onChange={(e) => setSelectedSectionFilter(e.target.value)}
                className="rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500 shadow-sm"
              >
                <option value="all">All Sections ({tools.length})</option>
                {sections.map((sec) => (
                  <option key={sec.id} value={sec.id}>
                    {sec.title?.en || sec.slug}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Tools Grid */}
          {(() => {
            const filteredTools = tools.filter((tool) => {
              if (selectedSectionFilter !== 'all' && tool.section_id !== selectedSectionFilter) {
                return false;
              }
              if (searchQuery.trim()) {
                const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase();
                const matchSpeaker = tool.speaker_name?.toLowerCase().includes(q);
                const matchTitle = Object.values(tool.title || {}).some(
                  (v) => typeof v === 'string' && v.toLowerCase().includes(q)
                );
                return matchSpeaker || matchTitle;
              }
              return true;
            });

            if (filteredTools.length === 0) {
              return (
                <div className="text-center py-12 rounded-3xl border border-dashed border-zinc-300 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50/50 dark:bg-zinc-900/50 p-6">
                  <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-500">No tools match your criteria.</p>
                </div>
              );
            }

            return (
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-3.5">
                {filteredTools.map((tool) => {
                  const section = sections.find((s) => s.id === tool.section_id);
                  return (
                    <ToolAdminCard
                      key={tool.id}
                      tool={tool}
                      sectionName={section?.title?.en || 'Section'}
                      onToggleFeatured={handleToggleToolFeatured}
                      onToggleVisibility={handleToggleToolVisibility}
                      onEdit={(t) => {
                        setEditingTool(t);
                        setToolModalOpen(true);
                      }}
                      onDelete={handleDeleteTool}
                    />
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            );
          })()}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Modals */}
      <SectionModal
        isOpen={sectionModalOpen}
        onClose={() => setSectionModalOpen(false)}
        onSave={handleSaveSection}
        section={editingSection}
      />

      <ToolModal
        isOpen={toolModalOpen}
        onClose={() => setToolModalOpen(false)}
        onSave={handleSaveTool}
        tool={editingTool}
        sections={sections}
      />
    </div>
  );
}
</file>

'use client';

import React from 'react';
import { Star, Eye, EyeOff, Edit, Trash2 } from 'lucide-react';
import type { Tool } from '@/types/app';

interface ToolAdminCardProps {
  tool: Tool;
  sectionName: string;
  onToggleFeatured: (tool: Tool) => void;
  onToggleVisibility: (tool: Tool) => void;
  onEdit: (tool: Tool) => void;
  onDelete: (tool: Tool) => void;
}

export function ToolAdminCard({
  tool,
  sectionName,
  onToggleFeatured,
  onToggleVisibility,
  onEdit,
  onDelete,
}: ToolAdminCardProps) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col justify-between p-4 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-all gap-3">
      <div>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-2 flex-wrap">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 flex-wrap">
            <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 dark:bg-amber-950/60 dark:text-amber-300">
              {sectionName}
            </span>
            <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-300 uppercase">
              {tool.type}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onToggleFeatured(tool)}
              className={`p-1.5 rounded-lg border transition-all ${
                tool.is_featured
                  ? 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-500 border-amber-300 dark:bg-amber-950/50 dark:border-amber-800'
                  : 'bg-zinc-50 text-zinc-400 border-zinc-200 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:border-zinc-700 hover:text-amber-500'
              }`}
              title={tool.is_featured ? 'Featured on top' : 'Click to feature'}
            >
              <Star className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${tool.is_featured ? 'fill-amber-500' : ''}`} />
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onToggleVisibility(tool)}
              className={`p-1.5 rounded-lg border transition-all ${
                tool.is_visible
                  ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 border-emerald-200 dark:bg-emerald-950/50 dark:border-emerald-800'
                  : 'bg-zinc-50 text-zinc-400 border-zinc-200 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:border-zinc-700'
              }`}
              title={tool.is_visible ? 'Visible' : 'Hidden'}
            >
              {tool.is_visible ? <Eye className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> : <EyeOff className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <h4 className="font-bold text-sm text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50 line-clamp-1">
          {tool.title?.en || 'Untitled'}
        </h4>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mt-1 text-xs text-zinc-500">
          {tool.speaker_name && <span>{tool.speaker_name}</span>}
          {tool.duration && <span>• {tool.duration}</span>}
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-1 mt-2.5 flex-wrap">
          {tool.available_languages?.map((lang) => (
            <span
              key={lang}
              className="px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 uppercase"
            >
              {lang}
            </span>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-1.5 pt-2 border-t border-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-800/80">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => onEdit(tool)}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 transition-colors"
        >
          <Edit className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          <span>Edit</span>
        </button>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => onDelete(tool)}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-red-600 dark:text-red-400 hover:bg-red-50 dark:hover:bg-red-950/40 transition-colors"
        >
          <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          <span>Delete</span>
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
