<file path="src/components/admin/ToolBasicInfo.tsx">
'use client';

import React from 'react';
import { Star, Eye, EyeOff, Check, Video, Music, FileText, Link as LinkIcon, Image as ImageIcon } from 'lucide-react';
import type { ToolType, LanguageCode, Section } from '@/types/app';

export const TOOL_TYPES: { type: ToolType; label: string; icon: React.ReactNode }[] = [
  { type: 'youtube', label: 'YouTube', icon: <Video className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-red-500" /> },
  { type: 'soundcloud', label: 'SoundCloud', icon: <Music className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-orange-500" /> },
  { type: 'video', label: 'Direct Video', icon: <Video className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-blue-500" /> },
  { type: 'pdf', label: 'PDF Document', icon: <FileText className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-500" /> },
  { type: 'image', label: 'Image', icon: <ImageIcon className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-500" /> },
  { type: 'link', label: 'Web Link', icon: <LinkIcon className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-purple-500" /> },
];

export const LANGUAGES: { code: LanguageCode; label: string; flag: string }[] = [
  { code: 'en', label: 'English', flag: '🇬🇧' },
  { code: 'bm', label: 'BM', flag: '🇲🇾' },
  { code: 'cn', label: '中文', flag: '🇨🇳' },
  { code: 'ta', label: 'Tamil', flag: '🇮🇳' },
];

export interface ToolBasicInfoProps {
  sections: Section[];
  sectionId: string;
  setSectionId: (id: string) => void;
  type: ToolType;
  setType: (type: ToolType) => void;
  speakerName: string;
  setSpeakerName: (name: string) => void;
  duration: string;
  setDuration: (dur: string) => void;
  isFeatured: boolean;
  setIsFeatured: (feat: boolean) => void;
  isVisible: boolean;
  setIsVisible: (vis: boolean) => void;
  availableLanguages: LanguageCode[];
  toggleLanguage: (code: LanguageCode) => void;
}

export function ToolBasicInfo(props: ToolBasicInfoProps) {
  const {
    sections,
    sectionId,
    setSectionId,
    type,
    setType,
    speakerName,
    setSpeakerName,
    duration,
    setDuration,
    isFeatured,
    setIsFeatured,
    isVisible,
    setIsVisible,
    availableLanguages,
    toggleLanguage,
  } = props;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <h3 className="text-xs font-bold text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
        1. Basic Information
      </h3>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3.5">
        <div>
          <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1.5">
            Section *
          </label>
          <select
            value={sectionId}
            onChange={(e) => setSectionId(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800/80 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500"
          >
            {sections.map((sec) => (
              <option key={sec.id} value={sec.id}>
                {sec.title?.en || sec.slug}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>

        <div>
          <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1.5">
            Media Type *
          </label>
          <select
            value={type}
            onChange={(e) => setType(e.target.value as ToolType)}
            className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800/80 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500"
          >
            {TOOL_TYPES.map((t) => (
              <option key={t.type} value={t.type}>
                {t.label}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>

        <div>
          <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1.5">
            Speaker / Author
          </label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={speakerName}
            onChange={(e) => setSpeakerName(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="e.g. Marcus Vance"
            className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800/80 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 placeholder:text-zinc-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500"
          />
        </div>

        <div>
          <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1.5">
            Duration / Format
          </label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={duration}
            onChange={(e) => setDuration(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="e.g. 5 mins, 📄 PDF (6 Pages)"
            className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800/80 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 placeholder:text-zinc-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500"
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 pt-1">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setIsFeatured(!isFeatured)}
          className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold border transition-all ${
            isFeatured
              ? 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-900 border-amber-300 dark:bg-amber-950/60 dark:text-amber-300 dark:border-amber-800'
              : 'bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-600 border-zinc-200 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:border-zinc-700'
          }`}
        >
          <Star className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${isFeatured ? 'fill-amber-500 text-amber-500' : ''}`} />
          <span>⭐ Featured at top</span>
        </button>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setIsVisible(!isVisible)}
          className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold border transition-all ${
            isVisible
              ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-900 border-emerald-300 dark:bg-emerald-950/60 dark:text-emerald-300 dark:border-emerald-800'
              : 'bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-500 border-zinc-200 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-500 dark:border-zinc-700'
          }`}
        >
          {isVisible ? <Eye className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> : <EyeOff className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
          <span>{isVisible ? 'Visible to Users' : 'Hidden from Users'}</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="pt-2">
        <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-2">
          Available Languages Checkboxes
        </label>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {LANGUAGES.map((l) => {
            const checked = availableLanguages.includes(l.code);
            return (
              <button
                key={l.code}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => toggleLanguage(l.code)}
                className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold border transition-all ${
                  checked
                    ? 'bg-zinc-900 text-white border-zinc-900 dark:bg-white dark:text-zinc-950 dark:border-white shadow-sm'
                    : 'bg-zinc-50 text-zinc-500 border-zinc-200 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:border-zinc-700'
                }`}
              >
                <span>{l.flag}</span>
                <span>{l.label}</span>
                {checked && <Check className="w-3 h-3 stroke-[3]" />}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/admin/ToolLangContent.tsx">
'use client';

import React from 'react';
import { Upload, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { LANGUAGES } from './ToolBasicInfo';
import type { LanguageCode } from '@/types/app';

export interface ToolLangContentProps {
  activeLangTab: LanguageCode;
  setActiveLangTab: (tab: LanguageCode) => void;
  availableLanguages: LanguageCode[];
  titles: Record<LanguageCode, string>;
  setTitles: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<Record<LanguageCode, string>>>;
  descriptions: Record<LanguageCode, string>;
  setDescriptions: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<Record<LanguageCode, string>>>;
  topicsStr: Record<LanguageCode, string>;
  setTopicsStr: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<Record<LanguageCode, string>>>;
  urls: Record<LanguageCode, string>;
  setUrls: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<Record<LanguageCode, string>>>;
  whatsappScripts: Record<LanguageCode, string>;
  setWhatsappScripts: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<Record<LanguageCode, string>>>;
  fileInputRef: React.RefObject<HTMLInputElement | null>;
  handleFileUpload: (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void;
  uploading: boolean;
}

export function ToolLangContent(props: ToolLangContentProps) {
  const {
    activeLangTab,
    setActiveLangTab,
    availableLanguages,
    titles,
    setTitles,
    descriptions,
    setDescriptions,
    topicsStr,
    setTopicsStr,
    urls,
    setUrls,
    whatsappScripts,
    setWhatsappScripts,
    fileInputRef,
    handleFileUpload,
    uploading,
  } = props;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4 pt-2 border-t border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <h3 className="text-xs font-bold text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
          2. Multilingual Content
        </h3>
        <span className="text-[11px] text-zinc-400">
          Editing: <strong className="text-amber-500 uppercase">{activeLangTab}</strong>
        </span>
      </div>

      <div className="flex rounded-2xl bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-800/80 p-1 border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800">
        {LANGUAGES.map((l) => {
          const isActive = activeLangTab === l.code;
          const isAvailable = availableLanguages.includes(l.code);
          return (
            <button
              key={l.code}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setActiveLangTab(l.code)}
              className={`flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold transition-all ${
                isActive
                  ? 'bg-white text-zinc-950 shadow-sm dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-50'
                  : 'text-zinc-600 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:text-zinc-200'
              }`}
            >
              <span>{l.flag}</span>
              <span>{l.label}</span>
              {isAvailable && (
                <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-500" title="Selected" />
              )}
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-3.5 bg-zinc-50/50 dark:bg-zinc-800/30 p-4 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-200/80 dark:border-zinc-800/80">
        <div>
          <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">
            Title ({activeLangTab.toUpperCase()}) {activeLangTab === 'en' && '*'}
          </label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={titles[activeLangTab] || ''}
            onChange={(e) => setTitles((prev) => ({ ...prev, [activeLangTab]: e.target.value }))}
            placeholder={`Enter ${activeLangTab.toUpperCase()} title...`}
            className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500"
          />
        </div>

        <div>
          <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">
            Short Description ({activeLangTab.toUpperCase()})
          </label>
          <textarea
            rows={2}
            value={descriptions[activeLangTab] || ''}
            onChange={(e) => setDescriptions((prev) => ({ ...prev, [activeLangTab]: e.target.value }))}
            placeholder={`Brief summary in ${activeLangTab.toUpperCase()}...`}
            className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500 resize-none"
          />
        </div>

        <div>
          <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">
            Topic Tags (comma-separated)
          </label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={topicsStr[activeLangTab] || ''}
            onChange={(e) => setTopicsStr((prev) => ({ ...prev, [activeLangTab]: e.target.value }))}
            placeholder="e.g. Cold Market, Scripts, Invitation"
            className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500"
          />
        </div>

        <div>
          <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">
            Media URL ({activeLangTab.toUpperCase()})
          </label>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <input
              type="text"
              value={urls[activeLangTab] || ''}
              onChange={(e) => setUrls((prev) => ({ ...prev, [activeLangTab]: e.target.value }))}
              placeholder="https://... or upload PDF / image"
              className="flex-1 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500"
            />
            <input
              ref={fileInputRef}
              type="file"
              onChange={handleFileUpload}
              className="hidden"
              accept=".pdf,.png,.jpg,.jpeg,.webp,.mp4,.mov,.mp3"
            />
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
              disabled={uploading}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-amber-500 text-white text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-amber-600 transition-colors disabled:opacity-50 flex-shrink-0 shadow-sm"
            >
              {uploading ? (
                <>
                  <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin" />
                  <span>Uploading...</span>
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <Upload className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>Upload File</span>
                </>
              )}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div>
          <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">
            Optional WhatsApp Share Script ({activeLangTab.toUpperCase()})
          </label>
          <textarea
            rows={2}
            value={whatsappScripts[activeLangTab] || ''}
            onChange={(e) => setWhatsappScripts((prev) => ({ ...prev, [activeLangTab]: e.target.value }))}
            placeholder="Pre-composed invitation pitch for one-tap WhatsApp sharing..."
            className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500 resize-none"
          />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

'use client';

import React from 'react';
import { Layers, Star, Calendar, Share2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { useApp } from '@/context/AppContext';

export type NavTab = 'tools' | 'saved' | 'calendar' | 'share';

interface BottomNavProps {
  activeTab: NavTab;
  onSelectTab: (tab: NavTab) => void;
  savedCount?: number;
}

export function BottomNav({ activeTab, onSelectTab, savedCount = 0 }: BottomNavProps) {
  const { t } = useApp();

  const tabs: { id: NavTab; labelKey: string; defaultLabel: string; icon: React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }> }[] = [
    { id: 'tools', labelKey: 'nav_tools', defaultLabel: 'Tools', icon: Layers },
    { id: 'saved', labelKey: 'nav_saved', defaultLabel: 'Saved', icon: Star },
    { id: 'calendar', labelKey: 'nav_calendar', defaultLabel: 'Calendar', icon: Calendar },
    { id: 'share', labelKey: 'nav_share_app', defaultLabel: 'Share App', icon: Share2 },
  ];

  return (
    <nav
      aria-label="Bottom Navigation"
      className="fixed bottom-0 left-0 right-0 z-40 border-t border-zinc-200 bg-white/95 backdrop-blur-lg dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950/95 transition-colors"
    >
      <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-xl items-center justify-around px-2 pt-2 pb-3 sm:pb-3">
        {tabs.map((tab) => {
          const isActive = activeTab === tab.id;
          const Icon = tab.icon;
          const label = t(tab.labelKey, tab.defaultLabel);

          return (
            <button
              key={tab.id}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onSelectTab(tab.id)}
              aria-label={label}
              aria-current={isActive ? 'page' : undefined}
              className={`relative flex flex-1 flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 rounded-2xl py-1.5 min-h-[52px] transition-all active:scale-95 ${
                isActive
                  ? 'text-zinc-950 dark:text-white font-bold'
                  : 'text-zinc-500 hover:text-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:text-zinc-200'
              }`}
            >
              <div className="relative">
                <div
                  className={`flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-xl transition-all ${
                    isActive
                      ? 'bg-zinc-900 text-white dark:bg-white dark:text-zinc-950 shadow-sm'
                      : 'bg-transparent text-current'
                  }`}
                >
                  <Icon className="h-4 w-4" />
                </div>
                {/* Saved Count Badge */}
                {tab.id === 'saved' && savedCount > 0 && (
                  <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1.5 flex h-4 min-w-[16px] items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-amber-500 px-1 text-[10px] font-black text-white shadow-xs">
                    {savedCount > 99 ? '99+' : savedCount}
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>
              <span className={`text-[11px] tracking-tight leading-none ${isActive ? 'font-bold' : 'font-medium'}`}>
                {label}
              </span>
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </nav>
  );
}

<file path="src/components/FilterBar.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useRef } from 'react';
import {
  Search,
  X,
  UserPlus,
  Rocket,
  Award,
  Sparkles,
  Layers,
  Compass,
  Folder,
  Tag,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { useApp, type ContentLangFilter } from '@/context/AppContext';
import type { Section } from '@/types/app';

interface FilterBarProps {
  searchQuery: string;
  onSearchChange: (q: string) => void;
  sections: Section[];
  activeSectionId: string;
  onSelectSection: (id: string) => void;
  contentLang: ContentLangFilter;
  onContentLangChange: (lang: ContentLangFilter) => void;
  topicTags: string[];
  activeTopic: string | null;
  onSelectTopic: (topic: string | null) => void;
  hasTamil?: boolean;
}

const SECTION_ICON_MAP: Record<string, React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>> = {
  UserPlus,
  Rocket,
  Award,
  Sparkles,
  Layers,
  Compass,
  Folder,
};

export function FilterBar({
  searchQuery,
  onSearchChange,
  sections,
  activeSectionId,
  onSelectSection,
  contentLang,
  onContentLangChange,
  topicTags,
  activeTopic,
  onSelectTopic,
  hasTamil = false,
}: FilterBarProps) {
  const { t, getLocalized } = useApp();
  const searchInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  const langFilters: { value: ContentLangFilter; label: string }[] = [
    { value: 'all', label: t('all', 'All') },
    { value: 'en', label: 'EN' },
    { value: 'bm', label: 'BM' },
    { value: 'cn', label: '中文' },
    ...(hasTamil ? [{ value: 'ta' as ContentLangFilter, label: 'தமிழ்' }] : []),
  ];

  return (
    <div className="w-full space-y-3 py-2">
      {/* Search Input Bar */}
      <div className="relative px-4">
        <div className="relative flex items-center">
          <Search className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3.5 h-4 w-4 text-zinc-400 dark:text-zinc-500" />
          <input
            ref={searchInputRef}
            type="text"
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={(e) => onSearchChange(e.target.value)}
            placeholder={t('search_placeholder', 'Search topic, speaker, or tool...')}
            className="h-11 w-full rounded-2xl border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 pl-10 pr-10 text-sm font-medium text-zinc-950 placeholder:text-zinc-400 focus:border-zinc-900 focus:bg-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-zinc-900 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 dark:placeholder:text-zinc-500 dark:focus:border-zinc-100 dark:focus:bg-zinc-950 dark:focus:ring-zinc-100 transition-all shadow-xs"
          />
          {searchQuery && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                onSearchChange('');
                searchInputRef.current?.focus();
              }}
              aria-label="Clear search"
              className="absolute right-3 flex h-6 w-6 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-zinc-200 text-zinc-600 hover:bg-zinc-300 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:bg-zinc-700"
            >
              <X className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
      {/* Horizontal Scrollable Section Pills */}
      <div className="no-scrollbar flex items-center gap-2 overflow-x-auto px-4 py-1">
        {/* All Section Pill */}
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => onSelectSection('all')}
          className={`flex flex-shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full px-4 py-2 text-xs font-bold transition-all duration-200 active:scale-95 ${
            activeSectionId === 'all'
              ? 'bg-zinc-900 text-white shadow-sm dark:bg-white dark:text-zinc-950'
              : 'bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-700 hover:bg-zinc-200 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-300 dark:hover:bg-zinc-700 border border-zinc-200/50 dark:border-zinc-700/50'
          }`}
        >
          <Layers className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
          <span>{t('all', 'All')}</span>
        </button>

        {/* Dynamic Section Pills */}
        {sections.map((sec) => {
          const isActive = activeSectionId === sec.id;
          const IconComp = SECTION_ICON_MAP[sec.icon_name] || Folder;
          const sectionTitle = getLocalized(sec.title) || sec.slug;

          return (
            <button
              key={sec.id}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onSelectSection(sec.id)}
              className={`flex flex-shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full px-4 py-2 text-xs font-bold transition-all duration-200 active:scale-95 ${
                isActive
                  ? 'bg-zinc-900 text-white shadow-sm dark:bg-white dark:text-zinc-950'
                  : 'bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-700 hover:bg-zinc-200 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-300 dark:hover:bg-zinc-700 border border-zinc-200/50 dark:border-zinc-700/50'
              }`}
            >
              <IconComp className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              <span>{sectionTitle}</span>
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {/* Sub-Filter Row: Compact Content Language Filter + Dynamic Topic Tags */}
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 px-4">
        {/* Content Language Pills */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto no-scrollbar py-0.5">
          <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-zinc-400 dark:text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-wider mr-1 flex-shrink-0">
            Lang:
          </span>
          {langFilters.map((lf) => {
            const isSelected = contentLang === lf.value;
            return (
              <button
                key={lf.value}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => onContentLangChange(lf.value)}
                className={`min-h-[28px] rounded-lg px-2.5 text-xs font-bold transition-all flex-shrink-0 ${
                  isSelected
                    ? 'bg-zinc-900 text-white dark:bg-white dark:text-zinc-900'
                    : 'bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-600 hover:bg-zinc-200 dark:bg-zinc-800/80 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:bg-zinc-700'
                }`}
              >
                {lf.label}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        {/* Dynamic Topic Tag Pills */}
        {topicTags.length > 0 && (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto no-scrollbar py-0.5">
            <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-zinc-400 dark:text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-wider mr-1 flex-shrink-0 flex items-center gap-1">
              <Tag className="h-3 w-3" />
            </span>
            {topicTags.map((topic) => {
              const isSelected = activeTopic === topic;
              return (
                <button
                  key={topic}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => onSelectTopic(isSelected ? null : topic)}
                  className={`min-h-[26px] flex-shrink-0 rounded-full px-3 text-[11px] font-semibold transition-all ${
                    isSelected
                      ? 'bg-amber-500 text-white shadow-xs font-bold'
                      : 'bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-600 hover:bg-zinc-200 dark:bg-zinc-800/80 dark:text-zinc-300 dark:hover:bg-zinc-700 border border-zinc-200/50 dark:border-zinc-700/50'
                  }`}
                >
                  #{topic}
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

<file path="src/components/ImageLightbox.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useEffect } from 'react';
import Image from 'next/image';
import { X, ExternalLink } from 'lucide-react';

interface ImageLightboxProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  imageUrl: string;
  title?: string;
}

export function ImageLightbox({ isOpen, onClose, imageUrl, title }: ImageLightboxProps) {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') onClose();
    };
    document.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    // Prevent body scroll when open
    const prevOverflow = document.body.style.overflow;
    document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
      document.body.style.overflow = prevOverflow;
    };
  }, [isOpen, onClose]);

  if (!isOpen || !imageUrl) return null;

  return (
    <div
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      aria-label={title || 'Image preview'}
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex flex-col items-center justify-center bg-black/90 p-4 backdrop-blur-md animate-in fade-in duration-200"
      onClick={onClose}
    >
      <div
        className="relative flex max-h-[92vh] max-w-2xl w-full flex-col items-center justify-center"
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      >
        {/* Top Controls */}
        <div className="mb-3 flex w-full items-center justify-between px-1 text-white">
          <p className="truncate text-sm font-semibold pr-4">{title || 'Preview'}</p>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <a
              href={imageUrl}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
              aria-label="Open image in new tab"
              className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-white/10 hover:bg-white/20 text-white transition-colors"
            >
              <ExternalLink className="h-4 w-4" />
            </a>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              aria-label="Close image"
              className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-white/10 hover:bg-white/20 text-white transition-colors"
            >
              <X className="h-5 w-5" />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Image Container */}
        <div className="relative max-h-[82vh] w-full overflow-hidden rounded-2xl bg-zinc-950 flex items-center justify-center border border-zinc-800">
          <Image
            src={imageUrl}
            alt={title || 'Enlarged view'}
            width={1200}
            height={900}
            unoptimized
            className="max-h-[82vh] w-auto max-w-full object-contain"
          />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/InstallAppBanner.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useEffect, useState, useSyncExternalStore } from 'react';
import Image from 'next/image';
import { Share, PlusSquare, X, Download } from 'lucide-react';
import { useApp } from '@/context/AppContext';

interface BeforeInstallPromptEvent extends Event {
  prompt: () => Promise<void>;
  userChoice: Promise<{ outcome: 'accepted' | 'dismissed'; platform: string }>;
}

const PWA_DISMISSED_KEY = 'teamhub_pwa_dismissed';
const PWA_EVENT = 'teamhub_pwa_event';

function notifyPwaChange() {
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined') window.dispatchEvent(new Event(PWA_EVENT));
}

function subscribePwa(callback: () => void) {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return () => {};
  window.addEventListener('storage', callback);
  window.addEventListener(PWA_EVENT, callback);
  return () => {
    window.removeEventListener('storage', callback);
    window.removeEventListener(PWA_EVENT, callback);
  };
}

function getDismissedSnapshot(): boolean {
  try {
    return localStorage.getItem(PWA_DISMISSED_KEY) === 'true';
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

function getStandaloneSnapshot(): boolean {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return true;
  return (
    window.matchMedia('(display-mode: standalone)').matches ||
    Boolean((navigator as unknown as { standalone?: boolean }).standalone)
  );
}

function getIsIosSnapshot(): boolean {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return false;
  return /iphone|ipad|ipod/.test(window.navigator.userAgent.toLowerCase());
}

export function InstallAppBanner() {
  const { t, isHydrated } = useApp();
  const [deferredPrompt, setDeferredPrompt] = useState<BeforeInstallPromptEvent | null>(null);

  const isDismissed = useSyncExternalStore(subscribePwa, getDismissedSnapshot, () => true);
  const isStandalone = useSyncExternalStore(subscribePwa, getStandaloneSnapshot, () => true);
  const isIOS = useSyncExternalStore(subscribePwa, getIsIosSnapshot, () => false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const onPrompt = (e: Event) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      setDeferredPrompt(e as BeforeInstallPromptEvent);
    };

    window.addEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', onPrompt);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', onPrompt);
  }, []);

  const handleDismiss = () => {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(PWA_DISMISSED_KEY, 'true');
      notifyPwaChange();
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('[InstallAppBanner] Persist failed:', err);
    }
  };

  const handleInstallClick = async () => {
    if (!deferredPrompt) return;
    try {
      await deferredPrompt.prompt();
      const choice = await deferredPrompt.userChoice;
      if (choice.outcome === 'accepted') handleDismiss();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('[InstallAppBanner] Prompt error:', err);
    } finally {
      setDeferredPrompt(null);
    }
  };

  if (!isHydrated || isDismissed || isStandalone || (!isIOS && !deferredPrompt)) {
    return null;
  }

  return (
    <aside
      aria-label="Install App"
      className="fixed bottom-4 left-3 right-3 sm:left-auto sm:right-6 sm:max-w-md z-50 animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-bottom-4 duration-300"
    >
      <div className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white/95 dark:bg-zinc-900/95 p-4 shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-md">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={handleDismiss}
          aria-label={t('close')}
          className="absolute top-3 right-3 p-1 rounded-full text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-500 dark:hover:text-zinc-200 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 transition-colors"
        >
          <X className="w-4 h-4" />
        </button>

        <div className="flex items-start gap-3.5 pr-6">
          <div className="relative w-12 h-12 rounded-xl overflow-hidden shadow-sm flex-shrink-0 bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-700/50">
            <Image
              src="/icons/icon.svg"
              alt="Team Hub"
              width={48}
              height={48}
              className="w-full h-full object-cover"
            />
          </div>
          <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
            <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 leading-snug">
              {t('install_app')}
            </h3>
            <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 leading-relaxed">
              {t('pwa_banner_desc')}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-3.5 pt-3 border-t border-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-800/80">
          {isIOS ? (
            <div className="space-y-1.5 text-xs text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className="flex h-5 w-5 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-800 text-[10px] font-bold text-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-200">
                  1
                </span>
                <span className="flex items-center gap-1 font-medium">
                  {t('pwa_ios_share_step')}
                  <Share className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-blue-500 inline-block" />
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className="flex h-5 w-5 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-800 text-[10px] font-bold text-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-200">
                  2
                </span>
                <span className="flex items-center gap-1 font-medium">
                  {t('pwa_ios_add_step')}
                  <PlusSquare className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 inline-block" />
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : deferredPrompt ? (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleInstallClick}
                className="flex-1 inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl bg-zinc-900 text-white dark:bg-white dark:text-zinc-950 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold shadow hover:bg-zinc-800 dark:hover:bg-zinc-100 transition-colors"
              >
                <Download className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                {t('add_to_home_screen')}
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleDismiss}
                className="px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:hover:text-zinc-200 transition-colors"
              >
                {t('dismiss')}
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400">
              <span className="font-medium">{t('add_to_home_screen')}</span>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleDismiss}
                className="font-medium text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 hover:underline"
              >
                {t('dismiss')}
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </aside>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/QRCodeModal.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { QRCodeSVG } from 'qrcode.react';
import { X, Copy, Check, ExternalLink, QrCode } from 'lucide-react';
import { useApp } from '@/context/AppContext';

interface QRCodeModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  title: string;
  speakerName?: string;
  language?: string;
  url: string;
}

export function QRCodeModal({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  title,
  speakerName,
  language,
  url,
}: QRCodeModalProps) {
  const { t } = useApp();
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') onClose();
    };
    document.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    const prevOverflow = document.body.style.overflow;
    document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
      document.body.style.overflow = prevOverflow;
    };
  }, [isOpen, onClose]);

  const handleCopy = async () => {
    try {
      if (navigator.clipboard) {
        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(url);
      } else {
        const textarea = document.createElement('textarea');
        textarea.value = url;
        document.body.appendChild(textarea);
        textarea.select();
        document.execCommand('copy');
        document.body.removeChild(textarea);
      }
      setCopied(true);
      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('Copy failed', err);
    }
  };

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      aria-labelledby="qr-modal-title"
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/80 p-4 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in duration-200"
      onClick={onClose}
    >
      <div
        className="relative w-full max-w-sm rounded-3xl bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 p-6 text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50 shadow-2xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800"
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      >
        {/* Close Button */}
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onClose}
          aria-label={t('close', 'Close')}
          className="absolute right-4 top-4 flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-600 hover:bg-zinc-200 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-300 dark:hover:bg-zinc-700 transition-colors"
        >
          <X className="h-4 w-4" />
        </button>

        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3 pr-8">
          <div className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100">
            <QrCode className="h-4 w-4" />
          </div>
          <span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
            {t('btn_qr', 'QR Code')}
          </span>
        </div>

        {/* Title & Metadata */}
        <div className="mb-4">
          <h2 id="qr-modal-title" className="text-base font-bold leading-snug line-clamp-2">
            {title}
          </h2>
          <div className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400">
            {speakerName && (
              <span className="inline-flex items-center font-medium">
                🎙️ {speakerName}
              </span>
            )}
            {language && (
              <span className="rounded-md bg-zinc-100 px-2 py-0.5 font-semibold text-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-200 uppercase">
                {language}
              </span>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* QR Code Container - High Contrast White Card */}
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-white p-5 shadow-inner border border-zinc-200">
          <QRCodeSVG
            value={url || 'https://teamhub.app'}
            size={220}
            level="M"
            marginSize={1}
            className="h-auto w-full max-w-[220px]"
          />
          <p className="mt-3 text-center text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-500">
            {t('scan_to_view', 'Scan with camera or phone to view')}
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Actions */}
        <div className="mt-4 flex gap-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleCopy}
            className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl bg-zinc-100 hover:bg-zinc-200 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:hover:bg-zinc-700 px-3 py-2.5 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors"
          >
            {copied ? (
              <>
                <Check className="h-4 w-4 text-emerald-500" />
                <span className="text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 font-bold">
                  {t('btn_copied', 'Copied!')}
                </span>
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <Copy className="h-4 w-4 text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400" />
                <span>{t('btn_copy', 'Copy Link')}</span>
              </>
            )}
          </button>

          <a
            href={url}
            target="_blank"
            rel="noopener noreferrer"
            className="flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl bg-zinc-900 text-white hover:bg-zinc-800 dark:bg-white dark:text-zinc-950 dark:hover:bg-zinc-100 px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors"
          >
            <ExternalLink className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            <span>{t('btn_open', 'Open')}</span>
          </a>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
</file>

'use client';

import React from 'react';
import {
  Search,
  X,
  Share2,
  Layers,
  UserPlus,
  Rocket,
  Award,
  Sparkles,
  Compass,
  Folder,
  BookOpen,
  Video,
  Calendar,
  Star,
  Megaphone,
  FileText,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { useApp, type ContentLangFilter } from '@/context/AppContext';
import type { Section } from '@/types/app';

export const SECTION_ICON_MAP: Record<string, React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>> = {
  UserPlus,
  Rocket,
  Award,
  Sparkles,
  Layers,
  Compass,
  Folder,
  BookOpen,
  Video,
  Calendar,
  Star,
  Megaphone,
  FileText,
};

interface SectionControlsProps {
  searchQuery: string;
  onSearchChange: (q: string) => void;
  visibleSections: Section[];
  activeSectionId: string;
  onSelectSection: (id: string) => void;
  contentLangFilter: ContentLangFilter;
  onContentLangChange: (lang: ContentLangFilter) => void;
  sectionToolCounts: Record<string, number>;
  totalToolsCount: number;
  activeSection: Section | null;
  activeSectionToolsCount: number;
  onShareFullSection: () => void;
  filteredCount?: number;
}

export function SectionControls({
  searchQuery,
  onSearchChange,
  visibleSections,
  activeSectionId,
  onSelectSection,
  contentLangFilter,
  onContentLangChange,
  sectionToolCounts,
  totalToolsCount,
  activeSection,
  activeSectionToolsCount,
  onShareFullSection,
  filteredCount = 0,
}: SectionControlsProps) {
  const { t, getLocalized } = useApp();

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3.5 pt-1">
      {/* 1) Single Smart Bar */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4">

            type="text"

          />
          {searchQuery && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onSearchChange('')}
              aria-label="Clear search"
              className="absolute right-2.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 flex h-6 w-6 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-zinc-200 text-zinc-600 hover:bg-zinc-300 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-400"
            >
              <X className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            </button>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Compact 3-Button Content Language Toggle */}
        <div className="flex items-center rounded-2xl bg-zinc-100 p-1 dark:bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-200/80 dark:border-zinc-800/80 flex-shrink-0">
          {[
            { id: 'all' as ContentLangFilter, label: t('all', 'All') },
            { id: 'en' as ContentLangFilter, label: 'EN' },
            { id: 'cn' as ContentLangFilter, label: '中文' },
          ].map((item) => {
            const isSelected = contentLangFilter === item.id;
            return (
              <button
                key={item.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => onContentLangChange(item.id)}
                className={`h-9 min-w-[36px] sm:min-w-[42px] px-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-all active:scale-95 ${
                  isSelected
                    ? 'bg-[#1E40AF] text-white shadow-xs'
                    : 'text-zinc-600 hover:text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:text-zinc-100'
                }`}
              >
                {item.label}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 2) Dynamic Section Selector Grid / Segmented Cards */}
      <div className="no-scrollbar flex items-center gap-2 overflow-x-auto px-4 py-1">
        {/* All Section Card */}
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => onSelectSection('all')}
          className={`flex flex-shrink-0 items-center gap-2 rounded-2xl px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold transition-all duration-200 active:scale-95 ${
            activeSectionId === 'all'
              ? 'bg-[#1E40AF] text-white shadow-sm ring-1 ring-[#1E40AF]'
              : 'bg-white text-zinc-700 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-300 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 border border-zinc-200/90 dark:border-zinc-800 shadow-xs'
          }`}
        >
          <div
            className={`flex h-6 w-6 items-center justify-center rounded-lg ${
              activeSectionId === 'all'
                ? 'bg-white/20 text-white'
                : 'bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-600 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-300'
            }`}
          >
            <Layers className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
          </div>
          <span>{t('all', 'All')}</span>
          <span
            className={`rounded-full px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-black ${
              activeSectionId === 'all'
                ? 'bg-white text-[#1E40AF]'
                : 'bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-600 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-400'
            }`}
          >
            {totalToolsCount}
          </span>
        </button>

        {/* Dynamic Visible Sections from Supabase */}
        {visibleSections.map((sec) => {
          const isActive = activeSectionId === sec.id;
          const IconComp = SECTION_ICON_MAP[sec.icon_name] || Folder;
          const sectionTitle = getLocalized(sec.title) || sec.slug;

                isActive
                  ? 'bg-[#1E40AF] text-white shadow-sm ring-1 ring-[#1E40AF]'
                  : 'bg-white text-zinc-700 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-300 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 border border-zinc-200/90 dark:border-zinc-800 shadow-xs'
              }`}
            >
              <div
                className={`flex h-6 w-6 items-center justify-center rounded-lg ${
                  isActive
                    ? 'bg-white/20 text-white'
                    : 'bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-600 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-300'
                }`}
              >
                <IconComp className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              </div>
              <span className="truncate max-w-[130px]">{sectionTitle}</span>
              <span
                className={`rounded-full px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-black ${
                  isActive
                    ? 'bg-white text-[#1E40AF]'
                    : 'bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-600 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-400'
                }`}
              >
                {count}
              </span>
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {/* 3) Section Header + "WhatsApp Full Section" Button */}
      {!searchQuery && (
        <div className="px-4">
          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-3xl bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-50/80 via-white to-blue-50/30 dark:from-zinc-900/90 dark:via-zinc-900 dark:to-blue-950/20 border border-blue-100/90 dark:border-zinc-800 p-4 shadow-xs">
            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-0.5">
                <h2 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-black tracking-tight text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50 truncate">
                  {activeSection
                    ? getLocalized(activeSection.title) || activeSection.slug
                    : t('all', 'All Resources')}
                </h2>
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-zinc-400 dark:text-zinc-500">
                  ({activeSectionToolsCount})
                </span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 line-clamp-2 leading-relaxed">
                {activeSection
                  ? getLocalized(activeSection.description) ||
                    'Browse curated resources and tools for this category'
                  : 'Access all team onboarding, prospecting, eBooks, and audios'}
              </p>
            </div>

            {/* WhatsApp Full Section Button */}
            {activeSectionToolsCount > 0 && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={onShareFullSection}
                className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-2xl bg-[#25D366] hover:bg-[#20bd5a] px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold text-white shadow-md shadow-emerald-500/20 active:scale-[0.98] transition-all flex-shrink-0 select-none"
              >
                <Share2 className="h-4 w-4" />
                <span>{t('btn_share_section', 'WhatsApp Full Section')}</span>
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Search Active Notification */}
      {searchQuery && (
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-4 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
          <span>
            Searching &quot;{searchQuery}&quot; across all sections ({filteredCount} results)
          </span>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onSearchChange('')}
            className="text-xs font-bold text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-400 hover:underline"
          >
            Clear search
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

<file path="src/components/ShareAppTab.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useState, useSyncExternalStore } from 'react';
import { QRCodeSVG } from 'qrcode.react';
import { Share2, Copy, Check, Smartphone } from 'lucide-react';
import { useApp } from '@/context/AppContext';

export function ShareAppTab() {
  const { t } = useApp();
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);

  const appUrl = useSyncExternalStore(
    () => () => {},
    () => (typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.location.origin : 'https://teamhub.app'),
    () => 'https://teamhub.app'
  );

  const appTitle = t('app_title', 'Team Hub');
  const shareDesc = t('share_app_desc', 'Scan this QR code with a phone camera or share via WhatsApp to onboard team members and prospects instantly.');

  const whatsappMessage = `${appTitle} - Super App\n\n${shareDesc}\n\n👉 ${appUrl}`;

  const handleWhatsAppShare = () => {
    window.open(`https://wa.me/?text=${encodeURIComponent(whatsappMessage)}`, '_blank', 'noopener,noreferrer');
  };

  const handleCopyLink = async () => {
    try {
      if (navigator.clipboard) {
        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(appUrl);
      } else {
        const textarea = document.createElement('textarea');
        textarea.value = appUrl;
        document.body.appendChild(textarea);
        textarea.select();
        document.execCommand('copy');
        document.body.removeChild(textarea);
      }
      setCopied(true);
      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('Copy error', err);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="w-full space-y-6 px-4 py-4 animate-in fade-in duration-200">
      {/* Title */}
      <div className="text-center">
        <div className="inline-flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-tr from-amber-500 to-orange-500 font-black text-white shadow-md mb-2">
          <span className="text-xl tracking-tighter">TH</span>
        </div>
        <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50">
          {t('share_app_title', 'Share Team Hub')}
        </h2>
        <p className="mt-1 text-xs sm:text-sm text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 max-w-sm mx-auto leading-relaxed">
          {shareDesc}
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Full-Screen / Prominent QR Code Card */}
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-sm overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 flex flex-col items-center">
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-white p-6 shadow-inner border border-zinc-200 w-full max-w-[270px]">
          <QRCodeSVG
            value={appUrl}
            size={230}
            level="Q"
            marginSize={1}
            className="h-auto w-full max-w-[230px]"
          />
          <p className="mt-3 text-center text-[11px] font-semibold text-zinc-500">
            {t('scan_to_view', 'Scan with camera or phone to view')}
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Action Buttons */}
        <div className="mt-6 w-full space-y-2.5">
          {/* WhatsApp Share Button */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleWhatsAppShare}
            className="flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-2xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white py-3.5 px-4 text-sm font-bold shadow-md transition-all active:scale-98"
          >
            <Share2 className="h-4 w-4" />
            <span>{t('btn_whatsapp', 'Share via WhatsApp')}</span>
          </button>

          {/* Copy Link Button */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleCopyLink}
            className="flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-2xl bg-zinc-100 hover:bg-zinc-200 text-zinc-900 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:hover:bg-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-100 py-3.5 px-4 text-sm font-bold transition-all active:scale-98"
          >
            {copied ? (
              <>
                <Check className="h-4 w-4 text-emerald-500" />
                <span className="text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 font-bold">
                  {t('btn_copied', 'Copied!')}
                </span>
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <Copy className="h-4 w-4" />
                <span>{t('btn_copy', 'Copy App Link')}</span>
              </>
            )}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Onboarding Tip Card */}
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-sm rounded-2xl bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-950/40 p-4 border border-amber-200 dark:border-amber-900/50 flex items-start gap-3">
        <div className="rounded-xl bg-amber-500 p-2 text-white flex-shrink-0">
          <Smartphone className="h-4 w-4" />
        </div>
        <div className="text-xs text-amber-900 dark:text-amber-200 leading-relaxed">
          <p className="font-bold mb-0.5">{t('add_to_home_screen', 'Instant Mobile App')}</p>
          <p>{t('pwa_banner_desc', 'Team members can install this app directly onto their phone home screens without the App Store.')}</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

<file path="src/context/AppContext.tsx">
'use client';

import React, {
  createContext,
  useCallback,
  useContext,
  useMemo,
  useSyncExternalStore,
  type ReactNode,
} from 'react';
import type { LanguageCode, LocalizedString, UiDictionary } from '@/types/app';
import { DEFAULT_LANGUAGE } from '@/types/app';
import { DEFAULT_UI_DICTIONARY } from '@/lib/dictionary';

export type ContentLangFilter = 'all' | LanguageCode;

export interface AppContextType {
  lang: LanguageCode;
  setLang: (lang: LanguageCode) => void;
  contentLangFilter: ContentLangFilter;
  setContentLangFilter: (filter: ContentLangFilter) => void;
  savedToolIds: string[];
  toggleSavedTool: (id: string) => void;
  isToolSaved: (id: string) => boolean;
  t: (key: string, fallback?: string) => string;
  getLocalized: (obj?: Partial<LocalizedString> | null, targetLang?: LanguageCode) => string;
  dictionary: Record<string, UiDictionary>;
  isHydrated: boolean;
}

const LANG_KEY = 'teamhub_lang';
const SAVED_TOOLS_KEY = 'teamhub_saved_tools';
const FILTER_KEY = 'teamhub_content_filter';
const STORAGE_EVENT = 'teamhub_storage_update';

function notifyStorageChange() {
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    window.dispatchEvent(new Event(STORAGE_EVENT));
  }
}

function subscribeStorage(callback: () => void) {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return () => {};
  window.addEventListener('storage', callback);
  window.addEventListener(STORAGE_EVENT, callback);
  return () => {
    window.removeEventListener('storage', callback);
    window.removeEventListener(STORAGE_EVENT, callback);
  };
}

let cachedSavedToolsRaw: string | null = null;
let cachedSavedTools: string[] = [];
const SERVER_SAVED_TOOLS: string[] = [];

function getSavedToolsSnapshot(): string[] {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(SAVED_TOOLS_KEY);
    if (raw !== cachedSavedToolsRaw) {
      cachedSavedToolsRaw = raw;
      if (raw) {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
        cachedSavedTools = Array.isArray(parsed)
          ? parsed.filter((item): item is string => typeof item === 'string')
          : [];
      } else {
        cachedSavedTools = [];
      }
    }
  } catch {
    cachedSavedTools = [];
  }
  return cachedSavedTools;
}

export function getLocalized(
  obj?: Partial<LocalizedString> | null,
  targetLang?: LanguageCode,
  activeLang: LanguageCode = DEFAULT_LANGUAGE
): string {
  if (!obj) return '';
  const langToUse = targetLang || activeLang;
  const val = obj[langToUse]?.trim();
  if (val) return val;

  if (obj.en?.trim()) return obj.en.trim();
  if (obj.bm?.trim()) return obj.bm.trim();
  if (obj.cn?.trim()) return obj.cn.trim();
  if (obj.ta?.trim()) return obj.ta.trim();
  return '';
}

export interface AppProviderProps {
  children: ReactNode;
  initialDictionary?: UiDictionary[] | Record<string, UiDictionary>;
  defaultLang?: LanguageCode;
  defaultContentFilter?: ContentLangFilter;
}

export function AppProvider({
  children,
  initialDictionary,
  defaultLang = DEFAULT_LANGUAGE,
  defaultContentFilter = 'all',
}: AppProviderProps) {
  const getLangSnapshot = useCallback((): LanguageCode => {
    try {
      const stored = localStorage.getItem(LANG_KEY);
      if (stored && ['en', 'bm', 'cn', 'ta'].includes(stored)) {
        return stored as LanguageCode;
      }
    } catch {}
    return defaultLang;
  }, [defaultLang]);

  const getFilterSnapshot = useCallback((): ContentLangFilter => {
    try {
      const stored = localStorage.getItem(FILTER_KEY);
      if (stored && ['all', 'en', 'bm', 'cn', 'ta'].includes(stored)) {
        return stored as ContentLangFilter;
      }
    } catch {}
    return defaultContentFilter;
  }, [defaultContentFilter]);

  const lang = useSyncExternalStore(subscribeStorage, getLangSnapshot, () => defaultLang);
  const contentLangFilter = useSyncExternalStore(
    subscribeStorage,
    getFilterSnapshot,
    () => defaultContentFilter
  );
  const savedToolIds = useSyncExternalStore(
    subscribeStorage,
    getSavedToolsSnapshot,
    () => SERVER_SAVED_TOOLS
  );
  const isHydrated = useSyncExternalStore(subscribeStorage, () => true, () => false);

  const dictionaryMap = useMemo<Record<string, UiDictionary>>(() => {
    const map: Record<string, UiDictionary> = { ...DEFAULT_UI_DICTIONARY };
    if (Array.isArray(initialDictionary)) {
      for (const row of initialDictionary) {
        if (row?.key) map[row.key] = row;
      }
    } else if (initialDictionary && typeof initialDictionary === 'object') {
      Object.assign(map, initialDictionary);
    }
    return map;
  }, [initialDictionary]);

  const setLang = useCallback((newLang: LanguageCode) => {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(LANG_KEY, newLang);
      notifyStorageChange();
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('[AppContext] Persist lang error:', err);
    }
  }, []);

  const setContentLangFilter = useCallback((filter: ContentLangFilter) => {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(FILTER_KEY, filter);
      notifyStorageChange();
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('[AppContext] Persist filter error:', err);
    }
  }, []);

  const toggleSavedTool = useCallback((id: string) => {
    const current = getSavedToolsSnapshot();
    const exists = current.includes(id);
    const next = exists ? current.filter((item) => item !== id) : [...current, id];
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(SAVED_TOOLS_KEY, JSON.stringify(next));
      notifyStorageChange();
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('[AppContext] Persist tools error:', err);
    }
  }, []);

  const isToolSaved = useCallback(
    (id: string) => savedToolIds.includes(id),
    [savedToolIds]
  );

  const t = useCallback(
    (key: string, fallback?: string): string => {
      const row = dictionaryMap[key];
      if (row) {
        const activeVal = row[lang]?.trim();
        if (activeVal) return activeVal;
        const enVal = row.en?.trim();
        if (enVal) return enVal;
        const bmVal = row.bm?.trim();
        if (bmVal) return bmVal;
        const cnVal = row.cn?.trim();
        if (cnVal) return cnVal;
        const taVal = row.ta?.trim();
        if (taVal) return taVal;
      }
      return fallback !== undefined ? fallback : key;
    },
    [dictionaryMap, lang]
  );

  const boundGetLocalized = useCallback(
    (obj?: Partial<LocalizedString> | null, targetLang?: LanguageCode): string => {
      return getLocalized(obj, targetLang, lang);
    },
    [lang]
  );

  const contextValue = useMemo<AppContextType>(
    () => ({
      lang,
      setLang,
      contentLangFilter,
      setContentLangFilter,
      savedToolIds,
      toggleSavedTool,
      isToolSaved,
      t,
      getLocalized: boundGetLocalized,
      dictionary: dictionaryMap,
      isHydrated,
    }),
    [
      lang,
      setLang,
      contentLangFilter,
      setContentLangFilter,
      savedToolIds,
      toggleSavedTool,
      isToolSaved,
      t,
      boundGetLocalized,
      dictionaryMap,
      isHydrated,
    ]
  );

  return <AppContext.Provider value={contextValue}>{children}</AppContext.Provider>;
}

export function useApp(): AppContextType {
  const context = useContext(AppContext);
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error('useApp must be used within an AppProvider');
  }
  return context;
}

export { DEFAULT_UI_DICTIONARY };

export const AppContext = createContext<AppContextType | undefined>(undefined);
</file>

<file path="src/types/app.ts">
/**
 * Team Hub - Application & Supabase Database Type Definitions
 */

export type LanguageCode = 'en' | 'bm' | 'cn' | 'ta';

export type LocalizedString = Record<LanguageCode, string>;

export type LocalizedStringArray = Record<LanguageCode, string[]>;

export type AccessPinRole = 'superadmin' | 'admin' | 'editor';

export type AccessPin = {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  pin_code: string;
  role: AccessPinRole;
  is_active: boolean;
  created_at: string;
};

export type UiDictionary = {
  key: string;
  en: string;
  bm: string;
  cn: string;
  ta: string;
  id?: string;
  created_at?: string;
};

export type Section = {
  id: string;
  slug: string;
  icon_name: string;
  sort_order: number;
  is_visible: boolean;
  title: LocalizedString;
  description: LocalizedString;
  created_at?: string;
  updated_at?: string;
};

export type ToolType = 'youtube' | 'soundcloud' | 'video' | 'pdf' | 'image' | 'link';

export type Tool = {
  id: string;
  section_id: string;
  type: ToolType;
  title: LocalizedString;
  description: LocalizedString;
  speaker_name: string;
  duration: string;
  available_languages: LanguageCode[];
  topics: LocalizedStringArray;
  url: LocalizedString;
  whatsapp_script: LocalizedString;
  thumbnail_url: string;
  is_featured: boolean;
  sort_order: number;
  is_visible: boolean;
  created_at?: string;
  updated_at?: string;
};

export type CalendarEvent = {
  id: string;
  date: string;
  time: string;
  title: LocalizedString;
  speaker: string;
  location_or_link: string;
};

export type TeamCalendar = {
  id: string;
  month_label: LocalizedString;
  image_url: LocalizedString;
  events: CalendarEvent[];
  created_at?: string;
  updated_at?: string;
};

/**
 * Supabase Database schema definitions
 */
export type Database = {
  public: {
    Tables: {
      access_pins: {
        Row: AccessPin;
        Insert: Omit<AccessPin, 'id' | 'created_at'> & {
          id?: string;
          created_at?: string;
        };
        Update: Partial<AccessPin>;
        Relationships: [];
      };
      ui_dictionary: {
        Row: UiDictionary;
        Insert: UiDictionary;
        Update: Partial<UiDictionary>;
        Relationships: [];
      };
      sections: {
        Row: Section;
        Insert: Omit<Section, 'id' | 'created_at' | 'updated_at'> & {
          id?: string;
          created_at?: string;
          updated_at?: string;
        };
        Update: Partial<Section>;
        Relationships: [];
      };
      tools: {
        Row: Tool;
        Insert: Omit<Tool, 'id' | 'created_at' | 'updated_at'> & {
          id?: string;
          created_at?: string;
          updated_at?: string;
        };
        Update: Partial<Tool>;
        Relationships: [];
      };
      team_calendar: {
        Row: TeamCalendar;
        Insert: Omit<TeamCalendar, 'id' | 'created_at' | 'updated_at'> & {
          id?: string;
          created_at?: string;
          updated_at?: string;
        };
        Update: Partial<TeamCalendar>;
        Relationships: [];
      };
    };
    Views: Record<string, never>;
    Functions: Record<string, never>;
  };
};

/**
 * Supported language metadata
 */
export const SUPPORTED_LANGUAGES: { code: LanguageCode; label: string; nativeLabel: string }[] = [
  { code: 'en', label: 'English', nativeLabel: 'English' },
  { code: 'bm', label: 'Malay', nativeLabel: 'Bahasa Melayu' },
  { code: 'cn', label: 'Chinese', nativeLabel: '中文' },
  { code: 'ta', label: 'Tamil', nativeLabel: 'தமிழ்' },
];

export const DEFAULT_LANGUAGE: LanguageCode = 'en';

export const PIN_SESSION_COOKIE = 'teamhub_admin_session';

export interface AdminSession {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  role: AccessPinRole;
}

export type ActionResponse<T = unknown> =
  | { success: true; data?: T; session?: AdminSession | null; url?: string; path?: string; message?: string }
  | { success: false; error: string; session?: null; data?: null };

export interface AdminDashboardData {
  session: AdminSession;
  sections: Section[];
  tools: Tool[];
  calendar: TeamCalendar;
  dictionary: UiDictionary[];
  pins?: AccessPin[];
}
</file>
