<file path="src/app/page.tsx">
import { supabase, supabaseAdmin } from '@/lib/supabase';
import type { Section, Tool, TeamCalendar } from '@/types/app';
import {
  DEFAULT_SECTIONS,
  DEFAULT_TOOLS,
  DEFAULT_CALENDAR,
} from '@/lib/mockData';
import { MainView } from '@/components/MainView';

async function getAppData(): Promise<{
  sections: Section[];
  tools: Tool[];
  calendar: TeamCalendar;
}> {
  "use cache";
  let sections: Section[] = DEFAULT_SECTIONS;
  let tools: Tool[] = DEFAULT_TOOLS;
  let calendar: TeamCalendar = DEFAULT_CALENDAR;

  try {
    const client = process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY ? supabaseAdmin : supabase;

    const [secRes, toolsRes, calRes] = await Promise.all([
      client.from('sections').select('*').order('sort_order', { ascending: true }),
      client.from('tools').select('*').order('sort_order', { ascending: true }),
      client.from('team_calendar').select('*').limit(1),
    ]);

    if (!secRes.error && secRes.data && secRes.data.length > 0) {
      sections = secRes.data as Section[];
    }

    if (!toolsRes.error && toolsRes.data && toolsRes.data.length > 0) {
      tools = toolsRes.data as Tool[];
    }

    if (!calRes.error && calRes.data && calRes.data.length > 0) {
      calendar = calRes.data[0] as TeamCalendar;
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('[Home page] Error loading Supabase data, using default seeds:', err);
  }

  return { sections, tools, calendar };
}

export default async function Home() {
  const { sections, tools, calendar } = await getAppData();

  return (
    <MainView
      initialSections={sections}
      initialTools={tools}
      initialCalendar={calendar}
    />
  );
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/admin/AdminPinLogin.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { Lock, Unlock, ArrowLeft, Delete, Sparkles, AlertCircle, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { verifyPinAction } from '@/app/actions/adminActions';
import { AALogo } from '@/components/AALogo';
import type { AdminSession } from '@/types/app';

const KEYPAD_BUTTONS = [
  { label: '1', value: '1' },
  { label: '2', value: '2' },
  { label: '3', value: '3' },
  { label: '4', value: '4' },
  { label: '5', value: '5' },
  { label: '6', value: '6' },
  { label: '7', value: '7' },
  { label: '8', value: '8' },
  { label: '9', value: '9' },
  { label: 'C', value: 'clear', isAction: true },
  { label: '0', value: '0' },
  { label: '⌫', value: 'backspace', isAction: true },
];

interface AdminPinLoginProps {
  onLoginSuccess: (session: AdminSession) => void;
}

export function AdminPinLogin({ onLoginSuccess }: AdminPinLoginProps) {
  const [pin, setPin] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [shake, setShake] = useState(false);

  const handleSubmit = useCallback(
    async (pinToVerify?: string) => {
      const targetPin = (pinToVerify !== undefined ? pinToVerify : pin).trim();
      if (!targetPin) {
        setError('Please enter your 6-digit access PIN');
        setShake(true);
        setTimeout(() => setShake(false), 500);
        return;
      }

      setLoading(true);
      setError(null);

      try {
        const res = await verifyPinAction(targetPin);
        if (res.success && res.session) {
          onLoginSuccess(res.session);
        } else {
          const errMsg = !res.success ? res.error : 'Invalid or deactivated PIN';
          setError(errMsg);
          setShake(true);
          setTimeout(() => setShake(false), 500);
          setPin('');
        }
      } catch (err) {
        setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Authentication service error');
        setShake(true);
        setTimeout(() => setShake(false), 500);
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    },
    [pin, onLoginSuccess]
  );

  const handleDigit = useCallback(
    (digit: string) => {
      if (loading) return;
      setError(null);
      setPin((prev) => {
        if (prev.length >= 8) return prev;
        const next = prev + digit;
        if (next.length === 6) {
          setTimeout(() => handleSubmit(next), 80);
        }
        return next;
      });
    },
    [loading, handleSubmit]
  );

  const handleBackspace = useCallback(() => {
    if (loading) return;
    setError(null);
    setPin((prev) => prev.slice(0, -1));
  }, [loading]);

  const handleClear = useCallback(() => {
    if (loading) return;
    setError(null);
    setPin('');
  }, [loading]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.target instanceof HTMLInputElement || e.target instanceof HTMLTextAreaElement) {
        return;
      }
      if (/^[0-9]$/.test(e.key)) {
        e.preventDefault();
        handleDigit(e.key);
      } else if (e.key === 'Backspace') {
        e.preventDefault();
        handleBackspace();
      } else if (e.key === 'Enter') {
        e.preventDefault();
        handleSubmit();
      } else if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        e.preventDefault();
        handleClear();
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
  }, [handleDigit, handleBackspace, handleSubmit, handleClear]);

  return (
    <div className="min-h-[85vh] flex flex-col items-center justify-center px-4 py-8">
      <div className="w-full max-w-sm mb-6 flex justify-between items-center">
        <Link
          href="/"
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-500 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:text-zinc-100 transition-colors"
        >
          <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          <span>View Live App</span>
        </Link>
        <span className="text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 uppercase tracking-widest">
          Secure Portal
        </span>
      </div>

      <div
        className={`w-full max-w-sm rounded-3xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white/90 dark:bg-zinc-900/90 backdrop-blur-xl p-6 sm:p-8 shadow-xl shadow-zinc-200/50 dark:shadow-black/50 transition-all ${
          shake ? 'animate-shake' : ''
        }`}
      >
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center text-center mb-6">
          <AALogo size="lg" className="mb-3 shadow-lg shadow-blue-900/25" />
          <h1 className="text-xl font-bold tracking-tight text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50">
            Admin Control Center
          </h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400 mt-1">
            Enter your 6-digit access PIN to unlock the dashboard
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* PIN Indicators */}
        <div className="mb-6 flex flex-col items-center">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-3">
            {[0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((idx) => (
              <div
                key={idx}
                className={`h-4 w-4 rounded-full transition-all duration-200 ${
                  pin.length > idx
                    ? 'bg-amber-500 scale-110 shadow-sm shadow-amber-500/40'
                    : 'border-2 border-zinc-300 dark:border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-800'
                }`}
              />
            ))}
          </div>

          {error && (
            <div className="mt-4 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-red-600 dark:text-red-400 bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-950/40 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full border border-red-200 dark:border-red-900/60">
              <AlertCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5 flex-shrink-0" />
              <span>{error}</span>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Sleek Numeric Keypad */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2.5 sm:gap-3 mb-5">
          {KEYPAD_BUTTONS.map((btn) => {
            const isBack = btn.value === 'backspace';
            const isClr = btn.value === 'clear';
            const handleClick = () => {
              if (isBack) handleBackspace();
              else if (isClr) handleClear();
              else handleDigit(btn.value);
            };

            return (
              <button
                key={btn.value}
                type="button"
                onClick={handleClick}
                disabled={loading}
                aria-label={btn.label}
                className={`flex h-13 sm:h-14 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl text-lg sm:text-xl font-semibold transition-all active:scale-95 disabled:opacity-50 select-none ${
                  btn.isAction
                    ? 'bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-800/60 text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 hover:bg-zinc-200 dark:hover:bg-zinc-700'
                    : 'bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800/90 text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50 border border-zinc-200/80 dark:border-zinc-700/80 hover:bg-white dark:hover:bg-zinc-700 shadow-sm'
                }`}
              >
                {isBack ? <Delete className="w-5 h-5" /> : btn.label}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        {/* Unlock Action Button */}
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => handleSubmit()}
          disabled={loading || pin.length === 0}
          className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500 to-orange-500 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white shadow-md shadow-orange-500/25 hover:from-amber-600 hover:to-orange-600 active:scale-[0.98] transition-all disabled:opacity-40 disabled:pointer-events-none"
        >
          {loading ? (
            <>
              <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" />
              <span>Verifying PIN...</span>
            </>
          ) : (
            <>
              <Unlock className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>Unlock Dashboard</span>
            </>
          )}
        </button>

        {/* Security Notice */}
        <div className="mt-5 pt-4 border-t border-zinc-200/80 dark:border-zinc-800/80 text-center">
          <p className="text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
            Protected Admin Portal • Authorized Access Only
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/admin/AdminTopBar.tsx">
'use client';

import React from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useTheme } from 'next-themes';
import {
  Crown,
  ShieldCheck,
  Edit,
  ExternalLink,
  LogOut,
  Sun,
  Moon,
  Sparkles,
} from 'lucide-react';
import type { AdminSession, AccessPinRole } from '@/types/app';
import { AALogo } from '@/components/AALogo';

interface AdminTopBarProps {
  session: AdminSession;
  onLogout: () => void;
  loggingOut?: boolean;
}

export function AdminTopBar({ session, onLogout, loggingOut }: AdminTopBarProps) {
  const { theme, setTheme, resolvedTheme } = useTheme();
  const [mounted, setMounted] = React.useState(false);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    setMounted(true);
  }, []);

  const isDark = mounted && (theme === 'dark' || resolvedTheme === 'dark');

  const roleConfig: Record<
    AccessPinRole,
    { label: string; badgeClass: string; icon: React.ReactNode }
  > = {
    superadmin: {
      label: 'Super Admin',
      badgeClass:
        'bg-purple-100 text-purple-800 border-purple-200 dark:bg-purple-950/70 dark:text-purple-300 dark:border-purple-800/60',
      icon: <Crown className="w-3.5 h-3.5 flex-shrink-0" />,
    },
    admin: {
      label: 'Admin',
      badgeClass:
        'bg-blue-100 text-blue-800 border-blue-200 dark:bg-blue-950/70 dark:text-blue-300 dark:border-blue-800/60',
      icon: <ShieldCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5 flex-shrink-0" />,
    },
    editor: {
      label: 'Editor',
      badgeClass:
        'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-200 dark:bg-emerald-950/70 dark:text-emerald-300 dark:border-emerald-800/60',
      icon: <Edit className="w-3.5 h-3.5 flex-shrink-0" />,
    },
  };

  const currentRole = roleConfig[session.role] || roleConfig.editor;

  return (
    <header className="sticky top-0 z-30 w-full border-b border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white/95 dark:bg-zinc-950/95 backdrop-blur-md transition-colors">
      <div className="mx-auto flex h-16 max-w-7xl items-center justify-between px-3 sm:px-6">
        {/* Brand & User identity */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 sm:gap-3 min-w-0">
          <AALogo size="md" />

          <div className="flex flex-col min-w-0">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className="truncate text-sm sm:text-base font-bold text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50">
                {session.label || 'Team Leader'}
              </span>

              {/* Role Badge */}
              <span
                className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[11px] font-semibold border ${currentRole.badgeClass}`}
              >
                {currentRole.icon}
                <span>{currentRole.label}</span>
              </span>
            </div>
            <span className="hidden sm:inline-block text-[11px] text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
              Admin Control Center
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Right actions: View Live App + Theme Toggle + Logout */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 sm:gap-2">
          {/* View Live App */}
          <Link
            href="/"
            target="_blank"
            rel="noopener noreferrer"
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-900 px-2.5 sm:px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 transition-colors"
            title="Open Live App in a new tab"
          >
            <ExternalLink className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-zinc-500" />
            <span className="hidden xs:inline sm:inline">View Live App</span>
            <span className="xs:hidden sm:hidden">Live</span>
          </Link>

          {/* Theme Toggle */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setTheme(isDark ? 'light' : 'dark')}
            className="flex h-8 w-8 sm:h-9 sm:w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-900 transition-colors"
            aria-label="Toggle theme"
          >
            {mounted ? (
              isDark ? (
                <Sun className="h-4 w-4 text-amber-400" />
              ) : (
                <Moon className="h-4 w-4 text-zinc-600" />
              )
            ) : (
              <span className="h-4 w-4" />
            )}
          </button>

          {/* Logout */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onLogout}
            disabled={loggingOut}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-950/50 border border-red-200 dark:border-red-900/60 px-2.5 sm:px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-red-600 dark:text-red-400 hover:bg-red-100 dark:hover:bg-red-900/40 transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"
            title="Log out of admin session"
          >
            <LogOut className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span className="hidden sm:inline">Logout</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/admin/SectionModal.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { X, Check, Folder, Loader2, AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import { LANGUAGES } from './ToolBasicInfo';
import type { Section, LanguageCode } from '@/types/app';

const COMMON_ICONS = [
  'UserPlus',
  'Rocket',
  'Award',
  'Sparkles',
  'Folder',
  'BookOpen',
  'Video',
  'Calendar',
  'Star',
  'Compass',
  'Megaphone',
  'FileText',
];

interface SectionModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSave: (section: Partial<Section>) => Promise<boolean>;
  section?: Section | null;
}

export function SectionModal({ isOpen, onClose, onSave, section }: SectionModalProps) {
  const [titles, setTitles] = useState<Record<LanguageCode, string>>({ en: '', bm: '', cn: '', ta: '' });
  const [descriptions, setDescriptions] = useState<Record<LanguageCode, string>>({ en: '', bm: '', cn: '', ta: '' });
  const [slug, setSlug] = useState('');
  const [iconName, setIconName] = useState('Folder');
  const [isVisible, setIsVisible] = useState(true);
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<LanguageCode>('en');

  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (section) {
      setTitles({
        en: section.title?.en || '',
        bm: section.title?.bm || '',
        cn: section.title?.cn || '',
        ta: section.title?.ta || '',
      });
      setDescriptions({
        en: section.description?.en || '',
        bm: section.description?.bm || '',
        cn: section.description?.cn || '',
        ta: section.description?.ta || '',
      });
      setSlug(section.slug || '');
      setIconName(section.icon_name || 'Folder');
      setIsVisible(section.is_visible !== false);
    } else {
      setTitles({ en: '', bm: '', cn: '', ta: '' });
      setDescriptions({ en: '', bm: '', cn: '', ta: '' });
      setSlug('');
      setIconName('Folder');
      setIsVisible(true);
    }
    setError(null);
    setActiveTab('en');
  }, [section, isOpen]);

  const handleSave = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!titles.en?.trim()) {
      setError('Section title in English is required.');
      setActiveTab('en');
      return;
    }

    setSaving(true);
    setError(null);

    const generatedSlug =
      slug.trim() ||
      titles.en
        .toLowerCase()
        .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-')
        .replace(/^-|-$/g, '') ||
      `section-${Date.now()}`;

    const payload: Partial<Section> = {
      ...(section?.id ? { id: section.id } : {}),
      slug: generatedSlug,
      icon_name: iconName,
      is_visible: isVisible,
      title: {
        en: titles.en.trim(),
        bm: titles.bm.trim() || titles.en.trim(),
        cn: titles.cn.trim() || titles.en.trim(),
        ta: titles.ta.trim() || titles.en.trim(),
      },
      description: {
        en: descriptions.en.trim(),
        bm: descriptions.bm.trim() || descriptions.en.trim(),
        cn: descriptions.cn.trim() || descriptions.en.trim(),
        ta: descriptions.ta.trim() || descriptions.en.trim(),
      },
    };

    const success = await onSave(payload);
    setSaving(false);
    if (success) {
      onClose();
    } else {
      setError('Failed to save section. Please check all fields.');
    }
  };

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-3 sm:p-4 bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in duration-200">
      <div className="relative w-full max-w-lg flex flex-col rounded-3xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 shadow-2xl overflow-hidden">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-5 sm:px-6 py-4 border-b border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50/70 dark:bg-zinc-900/70">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <div className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400">
              <Folder className="w-4 h-4" />
            </div>
            <h2 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50">
              {section ? 'Edit Section' : 'Add New Section'}
            </h2>
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-zinc-500 hover:bg-zinc-200 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 transition-colors"
          >
            <X className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSave} className="p-5 sm:p-6 space-y-4">
          {error && (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 p-3 text-xs text-red-600 dark:text-red-400 bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-950/40 border border-red-200 dark:border-red-900/60 rounded-2xl">
              <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 flex-shrink-0" />
              <span>{error}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3.5">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1.5">
                Icon Name
              </label>
              <select
                value={iconName}
                onChange={(e) => setIconName(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800/80 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500"
              >
                {COMMON_ICONS.map((icon) => (
                  <option key={icon} value={icon}>
                    {icon}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1.5">
                Custom Slug (Optional)
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={slug}
                onChange={(e) => setSlug(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="auto-generated-from-title"
                className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800/80 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 placeholder:text-zinc-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setIsVisible(!isVisible)}
              className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold border transition-all ${
                isVisible
                  ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-900 border-emerald-300 dark:bg-emerald-950/60 dark:text-emerald-300 dark:border-emerald-800'
                  : 'bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-500 border-zinc-200 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-500 dark:border-zinc-700'
              }`}
            >
              <span>{isVisible ? 'Section Visible to Users' : 'Section Hidden from Users'}</span>
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-3 pt-2 border-t border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800">
            <div className="flex rounded-2xl bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-800/80 p-1 border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800">
              {LANGUAGES.map((l) => (
                <button
                  key={l.code}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setActiveTab(l.code)}
                  className={`flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold transition-all ${
                    activeTab === l.code
                      ? 'bg-white text-zinc-950 shadow-sm dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-50'
                      : 'text-zinc-600 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:text-zinc-200'
                  }`}
                >
                  <span>{l.flag}</span>
                  <span>{l.label}</span>
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-3 bg-zinc-50/50 dark:bg-zinc-800/30 p-4 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-200/80 dark:border-zinc-800/80">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">
                  Title ({activeTab.toUpperCase()}) {activeTab === 'en' && '*'}
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={titles[activeTab] || ''}
                  onChange={(e) => {
                    const val = e.target.value;
                    setTitles({ ...titles, [activeTab]: val });
                    if (activeTab === 'en' && (!section || !slug || slug === titles.en.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/^-|-$/g, ''))) {
                      setSlug(val.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/^-|-$/g, ''));
                    }
                  }}
                  placeholder={`Section title in ${activeTab.toUpperCase()}...`}
                  className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">
                  Description ({activeTab.toUpperCase()})
                </label>
                <textarea
                  rows={2}
                  value={descriptions[activeTab] || ''}
                  onChange={(e) => setDescriptions({ ...descriptions, [activeTab]: e.target.value })}
                  placeholder={`Section description in ${activeTab.toUpperCase()}...`}
                  className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500 resize-none"
                />
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2.5 pt-3 border-t border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              disabled={saving}
              className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 transition-colors"
            >
              Cancel
            </button>
            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={saving}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500 to-orange-500 text-white text-xs font-bold hover:from-amber-600 hover:to-orange-600 transition-all shadow-md shadow-orange-500/20 disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              {saving ? (
                <>
                  <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin" />
                  <span>Saving...</span>
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>{section ? 'Update Section' : 'Create Section'}</span>
                </>
              )}
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/admin/TabCalendar.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useState, useRef } from 'react';
import {
  Calendar as CalendarIcon,
  Upload,
  Loader2,
  Check,
  Plus,
  Trash2,
  Edit,
  ExternalLink,
  ImageIcon,
  Sparkles,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { EventHighlightModal } from './EventHighlightModal';
import { LANGUAGES } from './ToolBasicInfo';
import { updateCalendarAction, uploadMediaAction } from '@/app/actions/adminActions';
import type { TeamCalendar, CalendarEvent, LanguageCode } from '@/types/app';

interface TabCalendarProps {
  calendar: TeamCalendar;
  setCalendar: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<TeamCalendar>>;
  onNotify: (msg: string, isError?: boolean) => void;
}

export function TabCalendar({ calendar, setCalendar, onNotify }: TabCalendarProps) {
  const [monthLabels, setMonthLabels] = useState(calendar.month_label || { en: '', bm: '', cn: '', ta: '' });
  const [imageUrls, setImageUrls] = useState(calendar.image_url || { en: '', bm: '', cn: '', ta: '' });
  const [events, setEvents] = useState<CalendarEvent[]>(calendar.events || []);

  const [activeLangTab, setActiveLangTab] = useState<LanguageCode>('en');
  const [isMasterImage, setIsMasterImage] = useState(true);

  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);

  // Modal
  const [eventModalOpen, setEventModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editingEvent, setEditingEvent] = useState<CalendarEvent | null>(null);

  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  const handleFileUpload = async (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;

    setUploading(true);
    try {
      const formData = new FormData();
      formData.append('file', file);
      const res = await uploadMediaAction(formData);

      if (res.success && res.url) {
        if (isMasterImage) {
          setImageUrls({
            en: res.url,
            bm: res.url,
            cn: res.url,
            ta: res.url,
          });
          onNotify('Flyer image uploaded and applied to all languages');
        } else {
          setImageUrls((prev) => ({ ...prev, [activeLangTab]: res.url || '' }));
          onNotify(`Flyer image uploaded for ${activeLangTab.toUpperCase()}`);
        }
      } else {
        onNotify('Image upload failed', true);
      }
    } catch {
      onNotify('Upload service error', true);
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
      if (fileInputRef.current) fileInputRef.current.value = '';
    }
  };

  const handleSaveCalendar = async () => {
    setSaving(true);
    const payload: Partial<TeamCalendar> = {
      ...(calendar.id ? { id: calendar.id } : {}),
      month_label: monthLabels,
      image_url: imageUrls,
      events,
    };

    const res = await updateCalendarAction(payload);
    setSaving(false);

    if (res.success && res.data) {
      setCalendar(res.data);
      onNotify('Monthly calendar saved successfully');
    } else {
      onNotify('Failed to save calendar', true);
    }
  };

  const handleSaveEvent = (savedEvent: CalendarEvent) => {
    setEvents((prev) => {
      const exists = prev.some((e) => e.id === savedEvent.id);
      if (exists) {
        return prev.map((e) => (e.id === savedEvent.id ? savedEvent : e));
      }
      return [...prev, savedEvent];
    });
    onNotify('Event highlight updated');
  };

  const handleDeleteEvent = (eventId: string) => {
    setEvents((prev) => prev.filter((e) => e.id !== eventId));
    onNotify('Event highlight removed');
  };

  const currentImageUrl = imageUrls[activeLangTab] || imageUrls.en || '';

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 pb-4">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50 flex items-center gap-2">
            <CalendarIcon className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-500" />
            <span>Monthly Calendar Management</span>
          </h2>
          <p className="text-xs text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400 mt-0.5">
            Update monthly flyer image and upcoming Zoom/event highlights.
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={handleSaveCalendar}
          disabled={saving}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500 to-orange-500 hover:from-amber-600 hover:to-orange-600 text-white text-xs font-bold shadow-md shadow-orange-500/20 transition-all active:scale-95 disabled:opacity-50 self-start sm:self-auto"
        >
          {saving ? (
            <>
              <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin" />
              <span>Saving Calendar...</span>
            </>
          ) : (
            <>
              <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Save Monthly Calendar</span>
            </>
          )}
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
        <div className="space-y-5">
          <div className="p-4 sm:p-5 rounded-3xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 shadow-sm space-y-3.5">
            <h3 className="text-xs font-bold text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
              1. Month Label (Multilingual)
            </h3>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              {LANGUAGES.map((l) => (
                <div key={l.code}>
                  <label className="block text-[11px] font-semibold text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 mb-1">
                    {l.flag} {l.label}
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={monthLabels[l.code] || ''}
                    onChange={(e) => setMonthLabels({ ...monthLabels, [l.code]: e.target.value })}
                    placeholder="e.g. November 2026"
                    className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500"
                  />
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="p-4 sm:p-5 rounded-3xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 shadow-sm space-y-4">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <h3 className="text-xs font-bold text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
                2. Monthly Calendar Flyer
              </h3>
              <label className="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer text-xs font-medium text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400">
                <input
                  type="checkbox"
                  checked={isMasterImage}
                  onChange={(e) => setIsMasterImage(e.target.checked)}
                  className="rounded text-amber-500 focus:ring-amber-500"
                />
                <span>One Master Image for All</span>
              </label>
            </div>

            {!isMasterImage && (
              <div className="flex rounded-xl bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-800 p-1 border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-700">
                {LANGUAGES.map((l) => (
                  <button
                    key={l.code}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setActiveLangTab(l.code)}
                    className={`flex-1 py-1 text-xs font-semibold rounded-lg transition-all ${
                      activeLangTab === l.code
                        ? 'bg-white text-zinc-950 shadow-sm dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-50'
                        : 'text-zinc-500 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-400'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {l.label}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}

            <div className="space-y-2">
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={imageUrls[activeLangTab] || ''}
                  onChange={(e) => setImageUrls({ ...imageUrls, [activeLangTab]: e.target.value })}
                  placeholder="https://... or upload flyer image"
                  className="flex-1 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500"
                />
                <input
                  ref={fileInputRef}
                  type="file"
                  onChange={handleFileUpload}
                  className="hidden"
                  accept=".jpg,.jpeg,.png,.webp,.pdf"
                />
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
                  disabled={uploading}
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-amber-500 text-white text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-amber-600 transition-colors disabled:opacity-50 flex-shrink-0 shadow-sm"
                >
                  {uploading ? (
                    <>
                      <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin" />
                      <span>Uploading...</span>
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      <Upload className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                      <span>Upload Flyer</span>
                    </>
                  )}
                </button>
              </div>

              {currentImageUrl ? (
                <div className="relative rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-800 max-h-56 flex items-center justify-center p-2">
                  {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
                  <img
                    src={currentImageUrl}
                    alt="Monthly Calendar Flyer"
                    loading="lazy"
                    className="w-full h-full object-contain max-h-52 rounded-xl"
                  />
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="h-36 rounded-2xl border-2 border-dashed border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-zinc-400 gap-1.5">
                  <ImageIcon className="w-6 h-6 text-zinc-300 dark:text-zinc-700" />
                  <span className="text-xs">No flyer image uploaded yet</span>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Right Column: Upcoming Event Highlights */}
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <div className="p-4 sm:p-5 rounded-3xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 shadow-sm space-y-4">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-xs font-bold text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
                  3. Upcoming Event Highlights ({events.length})
                </h3>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-zinc-400 mt-0.5">
                  Clickable Zoom meetings, trainings & rallies
                </p>
              </div>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  setEditingEvent(null);
                  setEventModalOpen(true);
                }}
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-600 text-white text-xs font-bold shadow-sm transition-all active:scale-95"
              >
                <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                <span>Add Event</span>
              </button>
            </div>

            {/* Events List */}
            {events.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="text-center py-10 rounded-2xl border-2 border-dashed border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 p-4">
                <p className="text-xs text-zinc-500">No event highlights scheduled yet.</p>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="space-y-2.5 max-h-[500px] overflow-y-auto pr-1">
                {events.map((ev) => (
                  <div
                    key={ev.id}
                    className="p-3.5 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50/50 dark:bg-zinc-800/40 hover:bg-zinc-50 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 transition-all flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-2.5"
                  >
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                        <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[10px] font-bold bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 dark:bg-amber-950/60 dark:text-amber-300">
                          {ev.date}
                        </span>
                        {ev.time && (
                          <span className="text-[11px] text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400 font-medium">
                            {ev.time}
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </div>

                      <h4 className="font-bold text-sm text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50 mt-1 line-clamp-1">
                        {ev.title?.en || 'Untitled Event'}
                      </h4>

                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mt-1 text-[11px] text-zinc-500 flex-wrap">
                        {ev.speaker && <span>Host: {ev.speaker}</span>}
                        {ev.location_or_link && (
                          <a
                            href={ev.location_or_link}
                            target="_blank"
                            rel="noopener noreferrer"
                            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-amber-600 hover:underline"
                          >
                            <span>Link / Map</span>
                            <ExternalLink className="w-3 h-3" />
                          </a>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1 self-end sm:self-center">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => {
                          setEditingEvent(ev);
                          setEventModalOpen(true);
                        }}
                        className="p-1.5 rounded-xl text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 hover:bg-zinc-200 dark:hover:bg-zinc-700 transition-colors"
                        title="Edit event"
                      >
                        <Edit className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                      </button>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleDeleteEvent(ev.id)}
                        className="p-1.5 rounded-xl text-red-600 dark:text-red-400 hover:bg-red-50 dark:hover:bg-red-950/40 transition-colors"
                        title="Remove event"
                      >
                        <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <EventHighlightModal
        isOpen={eventModalOpen}
        onClose={() => setEventModalOpen(false)}
        onSave={handleSaveEvent}
        event={editingEvent}
      />
    </div>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/admin/ToolModal.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import {
  X,
  Upload,
  Loader2,
  Check,
  Star,
  Eye,
  EyeOff,
  Video,
  FileText,
  Music,
  Link as LinkIcon,
  Image as ImageIcon,
  Sparkles,
  AlertCircle,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { uploadMediaAction } from '@/app/actions/adminActions';
import { ToolBasicInfo } from './ToolBasicInfo';
import { ToolLangContent } from './ToolLangContent';
import type { Tool, Section, ToolType, LanguageCode } from '@/types/app';

interface ToolModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSave: (tool: Partial<Tool>) => Promise<boolean>;
  tool?: Tool | null;
  sections: Section[];
}

const TOOL_TYPES: { type: ToolType; label: string; icon: React.ReactNode }[] = [
  { type: 'youtube', label: 'YouTube', icon: <Video className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-red-500" /> },
  { type: 'soundcloud', label: 'SoundCloud', icon: <Music className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-orange-500" /> },
  { type: 'video', label: 'Direct Video', icon: <Video className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-blue-500" /> },
  { type: 'pdf', label: 'PDF Document', icon: <FileText className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-500" /> },
  { type: 'image', label: 'Image', icon: <ImageIcon className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-500" /> },
  { type: 'link', label: 'Web Link', icon: <LinkIcon className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-purple-500" /> },
];

const LANGUAGES: { code: LanguageCode; label: string; flag: string }[] = [
  { code: 'en', label: 'English', flag: '🇬🇧' },
  { code: 'bm', label: 'BM', flag: '🇲🇾' },
  { code: 'cn', label: '中文', flag: '🇨🇳' },
  { code: 'ta', label: 'Tamil', flag: '🇮🇳' },
];

export function ToolModal({ isOpen, onClose, onSave, tool, sections }: ToolModalProps) {
  const [sectionId, setSectionId] = useState('');
  const [type, setType] = useState<ToolType>('link');
  const [speakerName, setSpeakerName] = useState('');
  const [duration, setDuration] = useState('');
  const [isFeatured, setIsFeatured] = useState(false);
  const [isVisible, setIsVisible] = useState(true);
  const [availableLanguages, setAvailableLanguages] = useState<LanguageCode[]>(['en']);

  const [activeLangTab, setActiveLangTab] = useState<LanguageCode>('en');

  const [titles, setTitles] = useState<Record<LanguageCode, string>>({ en: '', bm: '', cn: '', ta: '' });
  const [descriptions, setDescriptions] = useState<Record<LanguageCode, string>>({ en: '', bm: '', cn: '', ta: '' });
  const [urls, setUrls] = useState<Record<LanguageCode, string>>({ en: '', bm: '', cn: '', ta: '' });
  const [topicsStr, setTopicsStr] = useState<Record<LanguageCode, string>>({ en: '', bm: '', cn: '', ta: '' });
  const [whatsappScripts, setWhatsappScripts] = useState<Record<LanguageCode, string>>({ en: '', bm: '', cn: '', ta: '' });
  const [thumbnailUrl, setThumbnailUrl] = useState('');

  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (tool) {
      setSectionId(tool.section_id || sections[0]?.id || '');
      setType(tool.type || 'link');
      setSpeakerName(tool.speaker_name || '');
      setDuration(tool.duration || '');
      setIsFeatured(Boolean(tool.is_featured));
      setIsVisible(tool.is_visible !== false);
      setAvailableLanguages(tool.available_languages?.length ? tool.available_languages : ['en']);

      setTitles({ en: tool.title?.en || '', bm: tool.title?.bm || '', cn: tool.title?.cn || '', ta: tool.title?.ta || '' });
      setDescriptions({ en: tool.description?.en || '', bm: tool.description?.bm || '', cn: tool.description?.cn || '', ta: tool.description?.ta || '' });
      setUrls({ en: tool.url?.en || '', bm: tool.url?.bm || '', cn: tool.url?.cn || '', ta: tool.url?.ta || '' });
      setTopicsStr({
        en: (tool.topics?.en || []).join(', '),
        bm: (tool.topics?.bm || []).join(', '),
        cn: (tool.topics?.cn || []).join(', '),
        ta: (tool.topics?.ta || []).join(', '),
      });
      setWhatsappScripts({
        en: tool.whatsapp_script?.en || '',
        bm: tool.whatsapp_script?.bm || '',
        cn: tool.whatsapp_script?.cn || '',
        ta: tool.whatsapp_script?.ta || '',
      });
      setThumbnailUrl(tool.thumbnail_url || '');
    } else {
      setSectionId(sections[0]?.id || '');
      setType('youtube');
      setSpeakerName('');
      setDuration('');
      setIsFeatured(false);
      setIsVisible(true);
      setAvailableLanguages(['en']);
      setTitles({ en: '', bm: '', cn: '', ta: '' });
      setDescriptions({ en: '', bm: '', cn: '', ta: '' });
      setUrls({ en: '', bm: '', cn: '', ta: '' });
      setTopicsStr({ en: '', bm: '', cn: '', ta: '' });
      setWhatsappScripts({ en: '', bm: '', cn: '', ta: '' });
      setThumbnailUrl('');
    }
    setError(null);
    setActiveLangTab('en');
  }, [tool, sections, isOpen]);

  const toggleLanguage = (code: LanguageCode) => {
    setAvailableLanguages((prev) => {
      const exists = prev.includes(code);
      if (exists) {
        if (prev.length === 1) return prev;
        return prev.filter((l) => l !== code);
      } else {
        return [...prev, code];
      }
    });
  };

  const handleFileUpload = async (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;

    setUploading(true);
    setError(null);

    try {
      const formData = new FormData();
      formData.append('file', file);
      const res = await uploadMediaAction(formData);

      if (res.success && res.url) {
        setUrls((prev) => ({ ...prev, [activeLangTab]: res.url || '' }));
      } else {
        const msg = !res.success ? res.error : 'File upload failed';
        setError(msg);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Media upload error');
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
      if (fileInputRef.current) fileInputRef.current.value = '';
    }
  };

  const handleSave = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!titles.en?.trim()) {
      setError('English title is required.');
      setActiveLangTab('en');
      return;
    }
    if (!sectionId) {
      setError('Please select a section.');
      return;
    }

    setSaving(true);
    setError(null);

    const parseTopics = (str: string) =>
      str
        .split(',')
        .map((s) => s.trim())
        .filter(Boolean);

    const enTitle = titles.en.trim();
    const enUrl = urls.en.trim();
    const enDesc = descriptions.en.trim();
    const enScript = whatsappScripts.en.trim();

    const normalizedTitles: Record<LanguageCode, string> = {
      en: enTitle,
      bm: titles.bm.trim() || enTitle,
      cn: titles.cn.trim() || enTitle,
      ta: titles.ta.trim() || enTitle,
    };

    const normalizedUrls: Record<LanguageCode, string> = {
      en: enUrl,
      bm: urls.bm.trim() || enUrl,
      cn: urls.cn.trim() || enUrl,
      ta: urls.ta.trim() || enUrl,
    };

    const normalizedDescriptions: Record<LanguageCode, string> = {
      en: enDesc,
      bm: descriptions.bm.trim() || enDesc,
      cn: descriptions.cn.trim() || enDesc,
      ta: descriptions.ta.trim() || enDesc,
    };

    const normalizedScripts: Record<LanguageCode, string> = {
      en: enScript,
      bm: whatsappScripts.bm.trim() || enScript,
      cn: whatsappScripts.cn.trim() || enScript,
      ta: whatsappScripts.ta.trim() || enScript,
    };

    const payload: Partial<Tool> = {
      ...(tool?.id ? { id: tool.id } : {}),
      section_id: sectionId,
      type,
      speaker_name: speakerName.trim(),
      duration: duration.trim(),
      is_featured: isFeatured,
      is_visible: isVisible,
      available_languages: availableLanguages,
      title: normalizedTitles,
      description: normalizedDescriptions,
      url: normalizedUrls,
      topics: {
        en: parseTopics(topicsStr.en),
        bm: parseTopics(topicsStr.bm),
        cn: parseTopics(topicsStr.cn),
        ta: parseTopics(topicsStr.ta),
      },
      whatsapp_script: normalizedScripts,
      thumbnail_url: thumbnailUrl.trim(),
    };

    const success = await onSave(payload);
    setSaving(false);
    if (success) {
      onClose();
    } else {
      setError('Failed to save tool. Please check all fields.');
    }
  };

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-3 sm:p-4 bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in duration-200">
      <div className="relative w-full max-w-2xl max-h-[92vh] flex flex-col rounded-3xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 shadow-2xl overflow-hidden">
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-5 sm:px-6 py-4 border-b border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50/70 dark:bg-zinc-900/70">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <div className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400">
              <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4" />
            </div>
            <h2 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50">
              {tool ? 'Edit Tool' : 'Add New Tool'}
            </h2>
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-zinc-500 hover:bg-zinc-200 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 transition-colors"
          >
            <X className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSave} className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-5 sm:p-6 space-y-6">
          {error && (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 p-3 text-xs text-red-600 dark:text-red-400 bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-950/40 border border-red-200 dark:border-red-900/60 rounded-2xl">
              <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 flex-shrink-0" />
              <span>{error}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          <ToolBasicInfo
            sections={sections}
            sectionId={sectionId}
            setSectionId={setSectionId}
            type={type}
            setType={setType}
            speakerName={speakerName}
            setSpeakerName={setSpeakerName}
            duration={duration}
            setDuration={setDuration}
            isFeatured={isFeatured}
            setIsFeatured={setIsFeatured}
            isVisible={isVisible}
            setIsVisible={setIsVisible}
            availableLanguages={availableLanguages}
            toggleLanguage={toggleLanguage}
          />

          <ToolLangContent
            activeLangTab={activeLangTab}
            setActiveLangTab={setActiveLangTab}
            availableLanguages={availableLanguages}
            titles={titles}
            setTitles={setTitles}
            descriptions={descriptions}
            setDescriptions={setDescriptions}
            topicsStr={topicsStr}
            setTopicsStr={setTopicsStr}
            urls={urls}
            setUrls={setUrls}
            whatsappScripts={whatsappScripts}
            setWhatsappScripts={setWhatsappScripts}
            fileInputRef={fileInputRef}
            handleFileUpload={handleFileUpload}
            uploading={uploading}
          />

          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">
              Thumbnail Image URL (Optional)
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={thumbnailUrl}
              onChange={(e) => setThumbnailUrl(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="https://images.unsplash.com/... or uploaded cover"
              className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800/80 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2.5 pt-4 border-t border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              disabled={saving}
              className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 transition-colors"
            >
              Cancel
            </button>
            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={saving}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500 to-orange-500 text-white text-xs font-bold hover:from-amber-600 hover:to-orange-600 transition-all shadow-md shadow-orange-500/20 disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              {saving ? (
                <>
                  <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin" />
                  <span>Saving Tool...</span>
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>{tool ? 'Update Tool' : 'Create Tool'}</span>
                </>
              )}
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/CalendarTab.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import Image from 'next/image';
import {
  Calendar as CalendarIcon,
  Download,
  Share2,
  Video,
  MapPin,
  ExternalLink,
  Clock,
  Eye,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { useApp } from '@/context/AppContext';
import type { TeamCalendar } from '@/types/app';
import { ImageLightbox } from '@/components/ImageLightbox';

interface CalendarTabProps {
  calendar: TeamCalendar;
}

export function CalendarTab({ calendar }: CalendarTabProps) {
  const { t, getLocalized } = useApp();
  const [isLightboxOpen, setIsLightboxOpen] = useState(false);

  const monthLabel = getLocalized(calendar?.month_label) || t('upcoming_events', 'Team Calendar');
  const imageUrl = getLocalized(calendar?.image_url);
  const events = calendar?.events || [];

  const handleShareCalendar = () => {
    const shareText = `${monthLabel}\n\n${t('share_flyer', 'Check out our monthly team calendar and upcoming events:')}\n${imageUrl || (typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.location.href : '')}`;
    window.open(`https://wa.me/?text=${encodeURIComponent(shareText)}`, '_blank', 'noopener,noreferrer');
  };

  const handleDownloadFlyer = () => {
    if (imageUrl) {
      window.open(imageUrl, '_blank', 'noopener,noreferrer');
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="w-full space-y-6 px-4 py-4 animate-in fade-in duration-200">
      {/* Header */}
      <div>
        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold tracking-tight text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50 sm:text-2xl">
          {monthLabel}
        </h2>
        <p className="mt-1 text-xs sm:text-sm text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400">
          {t('tap_to_enlarge', 'Tap flyer to enlarge or share with your team')}
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Monthly Flyer Card */}
      {imageUrl ? (
        <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 shadow-sm">
          <div
            className="group relative aspect-[16/9] sm:aspect-[21/9] w-full cursor-pointer overflow-hidden bg-zinc-950"
            onClick={() => setIsLightboxOpen(true)}
          >
            <Image
              src={imageUrl}
              alt={monthLabel}
              fill
              unoptimized
              sizes="(max-width: 640px) 100vw, 640px"
              className="object-cover transition-transform duration-300 group-hover:scale-105"
            />
            <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-end justify-end p-3 bg-gradient-to-t from-black/60 via-transparent to-transparent">
              <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-black/75 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-white backdrop-blur-sm">
                <Eye className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                <span>{t('tap_to_enlarge', 'Tap flyer to enlarge')}</span>
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 p-3 border-t border-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-800">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleDownloadFlyer}
              className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-zinc-100 hover:bg-zinc-200 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:hover:bg-zinc-700 py-2.5 px-3 text-xs font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 transition-colors active:scale-95"
            >
              <Download className="h-4 w-4" />
              <span>{t('download_flyer', 'Download Flyer')}</span>
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleShareCalendar}
              className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white py-2.5 px-3 text-xs font-bold transition-colors active:scale-95 shadow-xs"
            >
              <Share2 className="h-4 w-4" />
              <span>{t('share_flyer', 'Share Calendar')}</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : null}

      {/* Upcoming Events Section */}
      <div className="space-y-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <CalendarIcon className="h-4 w-4 text-amber-500" />
          <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50">
            {t('upcoming_events', 'Upcoming Events')}
          </h3>
        </div>

        {events.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-zinc-300 p-8 text-center dark:border-zinc-800">
            <p className="text-sm text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
              {t('no_events', 'No upcoming events scheduled right now.')}
            </p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-3">
            {events.map((evt) => {
              const eventTitle = getLocalized(evt.title);
              const isUrl = evt.location_or_link.startsWith('http');
              const isZoom = evt.location_or_link.includes('zoom.us');

              const destinationUrl = isUrl
                ? evt.location_or_link
                : `https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=${encodeURIComponent(
                    evt.location_or_link
                  )}`;

              return (
                <div
                  key={evt.id}
                  className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-4 shadow-sm dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900"
                >
                  <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs font-bold text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400">
                      <span className="rounded-md bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-950/60 px-2 py-0.5">
                        {evt.date}
                      </span>
                      {evt.time && (
                        <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400 font-medium">
                          <Clock className="h-3 w-3" />
                          <span>{evt.time}</span>
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>

                    <h4 className="mt-1.5 text-sm sm:text-base font-bold text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50 leading-snug">
                      {eventTitle}
                    </h4>

                    {evt.speaker && (
                      <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400">
                        🎙️ <span className="font-medium">{evt.speaker}</span>
                      </p>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  {evt.location_or_link && (
                    <a
                      href={destinationUrl}
                      target="_blank"
                      rel="noopener noreferrer"
                      className="flex-shrink-0 inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl bg-zinc-900 text-white hover:bg-zinc-800 dark:bg-white dark:text-zinc-950 dark:hover:bg-zinc-100 px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold transition-all active:scale-95 shadow-xs"
                    >
                      {isZoom ? (
                        <>
                          <Video className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-blue-400 dark:text-blue-600" />
                          <span>Zoom</span>
                        </>
                      ) : isUrl ? (
                        <>
                          <ExternalLink className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                          <span>{t('join_event', 'Join')}</span>
                        </>
                      ) : (
                        <>
                          <MapPin className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-red-500" />
                          <span>{t('join_event', 'Location')}</span>
                        </>
                      )}
                    </a>
                  )}
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Lightbox for flyer */}
      {imageUrl && (
        <ImageLightbox
          isOpen={isLightboxOpen}
          onClose={() => setIsLightboxOpen(false)}
          imageUrl={imageUrl}
          title={monthLabel}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

<file path="src/components/Header.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useMemo, useSyncExternalStore } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useTheme } from 'next-themes';
import { Sun, Moon, Lock } from 'lucide-react';
import { useApp } from '@/context/AppContext';
import { AALogo } from '@/components/AALogo';
import type { LanguageCode } from '@/types/app';

export { AALogo };

export function Header() {
  const { lang, setLang, t, dictionary } = useApp();
  const { theme, setTheme, resolvedTheme } = useTheme();

  const mounted = useSyncExternalStore(
    () => () => {},
    () => true,
    () => false
  );

  // Show Tamil pill only if Tamil dictionary entries are populated
  const hasTamil = useMemo(() => {
    return Object.values(dictionary).some(
      (item) => typeof item.ta === 'string' && item.ta.trim().length > 0
    );
  }, [dictionary]);

  const languages: { code: LanguageCode; label: string }[] = useMemo(() => [
    { code: 'en', label: 'EN' },
    { code: 'bm', label: 'BM' },
    { code: 'cn', label: '中文' },
    ...(hasTamil ? [{ code: 'ta' as LanguageCode, label: 'தமிழ்' }] : []),
  ], [hasTamil]);

  const isDark = mounted && (theme === 'dark' || resolvedTheme === 'dark');

  const toggleTheme = () => {
    setTheme(isDark ? 'light' : 'dark');
  };

  return (
    <header className="sticky top-0 z-30 w-full border-b border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white/95 dark:bg-zinc-950/95 backdrop-blur-md transition-colors">
      <div className="mx-auto flex h-16 max-w-xl items-center justify-between px-4 sm:px-6">
        {/* App Title */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0">
          <AALogo size="md" />
          <h1 className="truncate text-lg font-bold tracking-tight text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50 sm:text-xl">
            {t('app_title', 'Team Hub')}
          </h1>
        </div>

        {/* Right Section: Language Switcher + Theme Toggle + Discreet Lock */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 sm:gap-2">
          {/* UI Language Pill Switcher */}
          <nav
            aria-label="UI Language Switcher"
            className="flex items-center rounded-full bg-zinc-100 p-0.5 dark:bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800"
          >
            {languages.map((l) => {
              const isActive = lang === l.code;
              return (
                <button
                  key={l.code}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setLang(l.code)}
                  aria-pressed={isActive}
                  className={`min-h-[34px] min-w-[36px] rounded-full px-2.5 text-xs font-semibold transition-all duration-200 active:scale-95 ${
                    isActive
                      ? 'bg-zinc-900 text-white shadow-sm dark:bg-white dark:text-zinc-950 font-bold'
                      : 'text-zinc-600 hover:text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:text-zinc-100'
                  }`}
                >
                  {l.label}
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </nav>

          {/* Dark / Light Mode Toggle */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={toggleTheme}
            aria-label={isDark ? 'Switch to light mode' : 'Switch to dark mode'}
            className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-zinc-600 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:bg-zinc-900 hover:text-zinc-950 dark:hover:text-zinc-100 transition-colors"
          >
            {mounted ? (
              isDark ? (
                <Sun className="h-4 w-4" />
              ) : (
                <Moon className="h-4 w-4" />
              )
            ) : (
              <span className="h-4 w-4" />
            )}
          </button>

          {/* Discreet Lock Icon linking to /admin */}
          <Link
            href="/admin"
            aria-label="Admin Portal"
            className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-500 dark:hover:text-zinc-200 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-900 transition-colors"
          >
            <Lock className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}

<file path="src/components/MainView.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import { useApp } from '@/context/AppContext';
import type { Section, Tool, TeamCalendar } from '@/types/app';
import { Header } from '@/components/Header';
import { ToolCard } from '@/components/ToolCard';
import { SectionControls } from '@/components/SectionControls';
import { BottomNav, type NavTab } from '@/components/BottomNav';
import { CalendarTab } from '@/components/CalendarTab';
import { ShareAppTab } from '@/components/ShareAppTab';
import {
  Star,
  FolderX,
  Search,
  X,
  Share2,
  Layers,
  UserPlus,
  Rocket,
  Award,
  Sparkles,
  Compass,
  Folder,
  BookOpen,
  Video,
  Calendar,
  Megaphone,
  FileText,
} from 'lucide-react';

const SECTION_ICON_MAP: Record<string, React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>> = {
  UserPlus,
  Rocket,
  Award,
  Sparkles,
  Layers,
  Compass,
  Folder,
  BookOpen,
  Video,
  Calendar,
  Star,
  Megaphone,
  FileText,
};

interface MainViewProps {
  initialSections: Section[];
  initialTools: Tool[];
  initialCalendar: TeamCalendar;
}

export function MainView({
  initialSections,
  initialTools,
  initialCalendar,
}: MainViewProps) {
  const {
    lang,
    getLocalized,
    contentLangFilter,
    setContentLangFilter,
    savedToolIds,
    t,
  } = useApp();

  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<NavTab>('tools');
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [activeSectionId, setActiveSectionId] = useState<string>('all');

  // Filter visible sections dynamically from Supabase
  const visibleSections = useMemo(() => {
    return (initialSections || []).filter((s) => s.is_visible !== false);
  }, [initialSections]);

  // Dynamic tool count per section
  const sectionToolCounts = useMemo(() => {
    const counts: Record<string, number> = { all: initialTools.length };
    visibleSections.forEach((sec) => {
      counts[sec.id] = initialTools.filter((t) => t.section_id === sec.id && t.is_visible !== false).length;
    });
    return counts;
  }, [visibleSections, initialTools]);

  // Current active section object (if specific section selected)
  const activeSection = useMemo(() => {
    if (activeSectionId === 'all') return null;
    return visibleSections.find((s) => s.id === activeSectionId) || null;
  }, [visibleSections, activeSectionId]);

  // Tools in the active section (before search query)
  const activeSectionTools = useMemo(() => {
    if (activeSectionId === 'all') {
      return initialTools.filter((t) => t.is_visible !== false);
    }
    return initialTools.filter(
      (t) => t.section_id === activeSectionId && t.is_visible !== false
    );
  }, [initialTools, activeSectionId]);

  // Real-time filtered tools list
  // If search query is present, search across ALL sections instantly by title or speaker name!
  const filteredTools = useMemo(() => {
    const q = searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase();

    // Search across ALL sections when user types in search bar
    const pool = q
      ? initialTools.filter((t) => t.is_visible !== false)
      : activeSectionTools;

    const result = pool.filter((tool) => {
      // 1. Content Language Filter
      if (
        contentLangFilter !== 'all' &&
        (!tool.available_languages || !tool.available_languages.includes(contentLangFilter))
      ) {
        return false;
      }

      // 2. Real-time Search Filter across Title and Speaker Name
      if (q) {
        const matchSpeaker = tool.speaker_name?.toLowerCase().includes(q);
        const matchTitle = Object.values(tool.title || {}).some(
          (v) => typeof v === 'string' && v.toLowerCase().includes(q)
        );
        const matchDesc = Object.values(tool.description || {}).some(
          (v) => typeof v === 'string' && v.toLowerCase().includes(q)
        );
        const matchTopics = Object.values(tool.topics || {}).some((arr) =>
          Array.isArray(arr) && arr.some((item) => item?.toLowerCase().includes(q))
        );

        if (!matchSpeaker && !matchTitle && !matchDesc && !matchTopics) {
          return false;
        }
      }

      return true;
    });

    // 3. Featured tools ALWAYS sort to the top, then by sort_order
    result.sort((a, b) => {
      if (a.is_featured !== b.is_featured) {
        return a.is_featured ? -1 : 1;
      }
      return (a.sort_order ?? 0) - (b.sort_order ?? 0);
    });

    return result;
  }, [
    initialTools,
    activeSectionTools,
    searchQuery,
    contentLangFilter,
  ]);

  // "WhatsApp Full Section" formatted sharing
  const handleShareFullSection = () => {
    const targetTools = activeSectionTools;
    if (targetTools.length === 0) return;

    const secTitle = activeSection
      ? getLocalized(activeSection.title) || activeSection.slug
      : t('all', 'All Resources');
    const secDesc = activeSection ? getLocalized(activeSection.description) : '';

    const header = `🔥 *${secTitle}*${secDesc ? `\n_${secDesc}_\n` : '\n'}\n`;

    const toolsList = targetTools
      .map((tool, idx) => {
        const toolTitle = getLocalized(tool.title) || 'Resource';
        const toolUrl = getLocalized(tool.url);
        const speaker = tool.speaker_name ? ` (${tool.speaker_name})` : '';
        return `${idx + 1}. *${toolTitle}*${speaker}\n👉 ${toolUrl}`;
      })
      .join('\n\n');

    const fullMsg = `${header}${toolsList}\n\n_Shared via AA Team Hub_`;
    const waUrl = `https://wa.me/?text=${encodeURIComponent(fullMsg)}`;
    window.open(waUrl, '_blank', 'noopener,noreferrer');
  };

  // Saved Tools List
  const savedTools = useMemo(() => {
    return initialTools
      .filter((tool) => savedToolIds.includes(tool.id))
      .sort((a, b) => {
        if (a.is_featured !== b.is_featured) {
          return a.is_featured ? -1 : 1;
        }
        return (a.sort_order ?? 0) - (b.sort_order ?? 0);
      });
  }, [initialTools, savedToolIds]);

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen flex-col bg-zinc-50 text-zinc-950 dark:bg-black dark:text-zinc-50 antialiased selection:bg-amber-500 selection:text-white">
      <Header />
      {/* Main Content Area */}
      <main className="mx-auto w-full max-w-xl flex-1 pb-28">
        {/* Tab 1: Tools (Main View) */}
        {activeTab === 'tools' && (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            <SectionControls
              searchQuery={searchQuery}
              onSearchChange={setSearchQuery}
              visibleSections={visibleSections}
              activeSectionId={activeSectionId}
              onSelectSection={setActiveSectionId}
              contentLangFilter={contentLangFilter}
              onContentLangChange={setContentLangFilter}
              sectionToolCounts={sectionToolCounts}
              totalToolsCount={initialTools.length}
              activeSection={activeSection}
              activeSectionToolsCount={activeSectionTools.length}
              onShareFullSection={handleShareFullSection}
              filteredCount={filteredTools.length}
            />

            <div className="space-y-4 px-4">
              {filteredTools.length > 0 ? (
                filteredTools.map((tool) => <ToolCard key={tool.id} tool={tool} />)
              ) : (
                <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-3xl border border-dashed border-zinc-300 dark:border-zinc-800 p-10 text-center bg-white/50 dark:bg-zinc-900/30">
                  <div className="flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-400 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-500 mb-3">
                    <FolderX className="h-6 w-6" />
                  </div>
                  <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100">
                    {t('empty_section', 'No tools match your filter.')}
                  </h3>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400 max-w-xs">
                    Try adjusting your search query or content language filter.
                  </p>
                  {(searchQuery || contentLangFilter !== 'all') && (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => {
                        setSearchQuery('');
                        setContentLangFilter('all');
                      }}
                      className="mt-4 rounded-xl bg-zinc-900 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-white hover:bg-zinc-800 dark:bg-white dark:text-zinc-950 transition-colors"
                    >
                      Clear Filters
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Tab 2: Saved (Starred Tools) */}
        {activeTab === 'saved' && (
          <div className="space-y-4 px-4 py-4 animate-in fade-in duration-200">
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-xl font-bold tracking-tight text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50 sm:text-2xl">
                {t('favorites', 'Saved Tools')}
              </h2>
              <p className="mt-1 text-xs sm:text-sm text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400">
                Quick access to tools you have starred for offline prospecting and review.
              </p>
            </div>

            {savedTools.length > 0 ? (
              <div className="space-y-4">
                {savedTools.map((tool) => (
                  <ToolCard key={tool.id} tool={tool} />
                ))}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-3xl border border-dashed border-zinc-300 dark:border-zinc-800 p-10 text-center bg-white/50 dark:bg-zinc-900/30">
                <div className="flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-amber-50 text-amber-500 dark:bg-amber-950/50 dark:text-amber-400 mb-3">
                  <Star className="h-7 w-7 fill-amber-400 text-amber-500" />
                </div>
                <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100">
                  {t('no_favorites', 'No saved tools yet.')}
                </h3>
                <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400 max-w-xs leading-relaxed">
                  Tap the star icon on any tool card in the Tools tab to save it here for instant access.
                </p>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setActiveTab('tools')}
                  className="mt-4 rounded-xl bg-zinc-900 px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold text-white hover:bg-zinc-800 dark:bg-white dark:text-zinc-950 transition-colors"
                >
                  Browse Tools
                </button>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Tab 3: Calendar */}
        {activeTab === 'calendar' && (
          <CalendarTab calendar={initialCalendar} />
        )}

        {/* Tab 4: Share App */}
        {activeTab === 'share' && <ShareAppTab />}
      </main>
      <BottomNav
        activeTab={activeTab}
        onSelectTab={setActiveTab}
        savedCount={savedToolIds.length}
      />
    </div>
  );
}
</file>
